Conclusion: 该框架为自适应优化算法提供了一个统一的理论基础，能够容纳多种流行方法并支持异构几何组合。提出的收敛分析在较弱的假设条件下成立，为实际应用中的自适应优化算法提供了理论保证。

Abstract: A unified framework for first-order optimization algorithms fornonconvex unconstrained optimization is proposed that uses adaptivelypreconditioned gradients and includes popular methods such as full anddiagonal AdaGrad, AdaNorm, as well as adpative variants of Shampoo andMuon. This framework also allows combining heterogeneous geometriesacross different groups of variables while preserving a unifiedconvergence analysis. A fully stochastic global rate-of-convergenceanalysis is conducted for all methods in the framework, with andwithout two types of momentum, using reasonable assumptions on thevariance of the gradient oracle and without assuming boundedstochastic gradients or small enough stepsize.

</details>


### [76] [Neural Adjoint Method for Meta-optics: Accelerating Volumetric Inverse Design via Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17425)
*Chanik Kang,Hyewon Suk,Haejun Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经伴随方法，使用傅里叶神经算子预测3D伴随梯度场，将元光学设计时间从数小时缩短到数秒


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元光学设计需要解决高维优化问题，传统方法需要迭代求解计算昂贵的麦克斯韦方程，即使使用伴随优化，宽带设计也需要数千次麦克斯韦求解，导致工业级优化缓慢且成本高昂

Method: 提出神经伴随方法，使用傅里叶神经算子从体素化介电常数体积预测3D伴随梯度场；引入阶段式FNO逐步细化残差误差，更强调高频分量以保留灵敏度峰值；构建了来自配对前向/伴随FDTD模拟的元光学数据集

Result: 方法在三个任务上评估：光谱分选（颜色路由器）、消色差聚焦（超透镜）和波导模式转换；将设计时间从数小时减少到数秒

Conclusion: 该方法为快速、大规模体积元光学设计提供了一条实用途径，通过AI加速科学计算实现

Abstract: Meta-optics promises compact, high-performance imaging and color routing. However, designing high-performance structures is a high-dimensional optimization problem: mapping a desired optical output back to a physical 3D structure requires solving computationally expensive Maxwell's equations iteratively. Even with adjoint optimization, broadband design can require thousands of Maxwell solves, making industrial-scale optimization slow and costly. To overcome this challenge, we propose the Neural Adjoint Method, a solver-supervised surrogate that predicts 3D adjoint gradient fields from a voxelized permittivity volume using a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO). By learning the dense, per-voxel sensitivity field that drives gradient-based updates, our method can replace per-iteration adjoint solves with fast predictions, greatly reducing the computational cost of full-wave simulations required during iterative refinement. To better preserve sensitivity peaks, we introduce a stage-wise FNO that progressively refines residual errors with increasing emphasis on higher-frequency components. We curate a meta-optics dataset from paired forward/adjoint FDTD simulations and evaluate it across three tasks: spectral sorting (color routers), achromatic focusing (metalenses), and waveguide mode conversion. Our method reduces design time from hours to seconds. These results suggest a practical route toward fast, large-scale volumetric meta-optical design enabled by AI-accelerated scientific computing.

</details>


### [77] [Trustworthy deep domain adaptation for wearable photoplethysmography signal analysis with decision-theoretic uncertainty quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17480)
*Ciaran Bench*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用决策理论的不确定性量化来评估生成模型在领域适应中的输出可信度，解决了传统评估方法在缺乏真实标签时无法有效评估生成质量的问题，并通过光电容积脉搏波去噪案例验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型可用于领域适应，但容易产生幻觉和伪影，影响下游判别模型的性能。传统不确定性量化评估方法在缺乏真实标签时难以适用，且评估结果与下游任务的实际效用脱节。

Method: 采用决策理论的不确定性量化框架来评估生成输出的可信度，特别针对领域适应场景。该方法将下游分类器用于评估生成输出质量的形式化，通过光电容积脉搏波时间序列去噪用于心房颤动分类的案例研究进行验证。

Result: 决策理论的不确定性量化提供了一种评估生成输出可信度的便捷框架，能够更好地反映不确定性在实际应用场景中的效用，解决了传统评估方法在缺乏真实标签时的局限性。

Conclusion: 决策理论的不确定性量化为评估生成模型在领域适应中的输出质量提供了有效的理论框架，将下游任务性能与不确定性评估相结合，为生成输出的可信度评估提供了更实用的方法。

Abstract: In principle, deep generative models can be used to perform domain adaptation; i.e. align the input feature representations of test data with that of a separate discriminative model's training data. This can help improve the discriminative model's performance on the test data. However, generative models are prone to producing hallucinations and artefacts that may degrade the quality of generated data, and therefore, predictive performance when processed by the discriminative model. While uncertainty quantification can provide a means to assess the quality of adapted data, the standard framework for evaluating the quality of predicted uncertainties may not easily extend to generative models due to the common lack of ground truths (among other reasons). Even with ground truths, this evaluation is agnostic to how the generated outputs are used on the downstream task, limiting the extent to which the uncertainty reliability analysis provides insights about the utility of the uncertainties with respect to the intended use case of the adapted examples. Here, we describe how decision-theoretic uncertainty quantification can address these concerns and provide a convenient framework for evaluating the trustworthiness of generated outputs, in particular, for domain adaptation. We consider a case study in photoplethysmography time series denoising for Atrial Fibrillation classification. This formalises a well-known heuristic method of using a downstream classifier to assess the quality of generated outputs.

</details>


### [78] [A Probabilistic Consensus-Driven Approach for Robust Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17494)
*Marcin Kostrzewa,Maciej Zięba,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的鲁棒反事实解释方法，通过联合建模数据分布和模型决策空间，使用条件归一化流在模型集成上建立概率共识，实现可调节的鲁棒性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成鲁棒反事实解释的方法存在局限性：通常只适用于特定模型类型、需要昂贵的调优、或缺乏灵活的鲁棒性控制。当模型轻微变化时，反事实解释容易失效。

Method: 使用模型集成建立概率共识，训练条件归一化流来捕捉不同分类器一致性水平下的数据密度。在推理时，通过单个可解释参数控制鲁棒性水平，指定目标类别所需的最小模型共识比例。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在保持良好综合性能的同时，实现了优越的经验鲁棒性。能够有效将反事实解释推向既合理又在模型变化下稳定的区域。

Conclusion: 提出的方法通过联合建模数据分布和模型决策空间，提供了一种灵活且有效的鲁棒反事实解释生成方案，解决了现有方法在模型变化下的脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CFEs) are essential for interpreting black-box models, yet they often become invalid when models are slightly changed. Existing methods for generating robust CFEs are often limited to specific types of models, require costly tuning, or inflexible robustness controls. We propose a novel approach that jointly models the data distribution and the space of plausible model decisions to ensure robustness to model changes. Using a probabilistic consensus over a model ensemble, we train a conditional normalizing flow that captures the data density under varying levels of classifier agreement. At inference time, a single interpretable parameter controls the robustness level; it specifies the minimum fraction of models that should agree on the target class without retraining the generative model. Our method effectively pushes CFEs toward regions that are both plausible and stable across model changes. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves superior empirical robustness while also maintaining good performance across other evaluation measures.

</details>


### [79] [SVL: Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning as Survival Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17551)
*Franki Nguimatsia Tiofack,Fabian Schramm,Théotime Le Hellard,Justin Carpentier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SVL将目标条件强化学习重构为生存学习问题，通过建模状态到目标的时间分布，避免了时序差分学习的不稳定性，在长视野任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于时序差分学习的目标条件强化学习方法存在不稳定性和样本效率低的问题，需要更稳定、更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出生存价值学习(SVL)，将GCRL重构为生存学习问题，将状态到目标的时间建模为概率分布，通过生存概率的折现和表达价值函数，使用最大似然训练风险模型。

Result: 在离线GCRL基准测试中，SVL结合分层行动器匹配或超越了强基线方法，在复杂长视野任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: SVL提供了一种稳定、高效的GCRL替代方法，特别适用于长视野任务，通过生存学习框架避免了时序差分学习的不稳定性。

Abstract: Standard approaches to goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) that rely on temporal-difference learning can be unstable and sample-inefficient due to bootstrapping. While recent work has explored contrastive and supervised formulations to improve stability, we present a probabilistic alternative, called survival value learning (SVL), that reframes GCRL as a survival learning problem by modeling the time-to-goal from each state as a probability distribution. This structured distributional Monte Carlo perspective yields a closed-form identity that expresses the goal-conditioned value function as a discounted sum of survival probabilities, enabling value estimation via a hazard model trained via maximum likelihood on both event and right-censored trajectories. We introduce three practical value estimators, including finite-horizon truncation and two binned infinite-horizon approximations to capture long-horizon objectives. Experiments on offline GCRL benchmarks show that SVL combined with hierarchical actors matches or surpasses strong hierarchical TD and Monte Carlo baselines, excelling on complex, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Diverse Dictionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17568)
*Yujia Zheng,Zijian Li,Shunxing Fan,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出多样化字典学习问题，在无法完全识别潜变量的情况下，通过集合论方法识别潜变量的交集、补集、对称差等结构化部分，并利用简单归纳偏置实现可验证的恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要线性假设或辅助监督等强假设，这些假设在实践中难以验证且容易失效。在无法完全识别潜变量的现实场景中，需要探索在弱假设下仍能保证恢复的内容以及可普遍采用的偏置。

Method: 引入多样化字典学习问题，证明即使没有强假设，与任意观测相关的潜变量的交集、补集、对称差以及潜变量到观测的依赖结构仍可在适当不确定性下识别。这些集合论结果可通过集合代数组合构建隐藏世界的结构化视图。

Result: 理论证明在存在足够结构多样性的情况下可实现所有潜变量的完全识别。所有识别优势都源于估计过程中的简单归纳偏置，该偏置可轻松集成到大多数模型中。在合成和真实数据上验证了理论并展示了偏置的益处。

Conclusion: 提出了一种互补视角：在无法完全识别潜变量的情况下，通过集合论方法恢复可保证的结构化部分。这种方法不依赖难以验证的强假设，通过简单归纳偏置即可获得可验证的恢复保证，为现实世界场景中的可识别性提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Given only observational data $X = g(Z)$, where both the latent variables $Z$ and the generating process $g$ are unknown, recovering $Z$ is ill-posed without additional assumptions. Existing methods often assume linearity or rely on auxiliary supervision and functional constraints. However, such assumptions are rarely verifiable in practice, and most theoretical guarantees break down under even mild violations, leaving uncertainty about how to reliably understand the hidden world. To make identifiability actionable in the real-world scenarios, we take a complementary view: in the general settings where full identifiability is unattainable, what can still be recovered with guarantees, and what biases could be universally adopted? We introduce the problem of diverse dictionary learning to formalize this view. Specifically, we show that intersections, complements, and symmetric differences of latent variables linked to arbitrary observations, along with the latent-to-observed dependency structure, are still identifiable up to appropriate indeterminacies even without strong assumptions. These set-theoretic results can be composed using set algebra to construct structured and essential views of the hidden world, such as genus-differentia definitions. When sufficient structural diversity is present, they further imply full identifiability of all latent variables. Notably, all identifiability benefits follow from a simple inductive bias during estimation that can be readily integrated into most models. We validate the theory and demonstrate the benefits of the bias on both synthetic and real-world data.

</details>


### [81] [Recovery Guarantees for Continual Learning of Dependent Tasks: Memory, Data-Dependent Regularization, and Data-Dependent Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17578)
*Liangzu Peng,Uday Kiran Reddy Tadipatri,Ziqing Xu,Eric Eaton,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为持续学习理论分析提供了首个非平凡统计恢复保证，通过建模任务间依赖关系，证明了多种实际CL方法的误差边界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习理论研究滞后于实证进展，核心挑战在于任务间数据分布变化。现有理论分析在任务依赖建模方面不足，导致边界信息量有限或空洞

Method: 提出非线性回归任务依赖模型：当前任务数据是先前数据的非线性变换。在此模型和自然假设下，分析多种CL范式的统计恢复保证，包括经验回放（含数据无关正则化/权重平衡）、数据相关权重回放、数据相关正则化（如知识蒸馏）

Result: 证明了多种持续学习方法的非平凡估计误差边界，这些边界在先前工作给出空洞边界的情况下仍具有信息量。为经验回放、权重平衡、知识蒸馏等实际CL方法提供了首个理论保证

Conclusion: 通过显式建模任务依赖关系，为持续学习理论分析开辟了新方向，为实际CL方法提供了有意义的统计保证，填补了理论与实证之间的差距

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is concerned with learning multiple tasks sequentially without forgetting previously learned tasks. Despite substantial empirical advances over recent years, the theoretical development of CL remains in its infancy. At the heart of developing CL theory lies the challenge that the data distribution varies across tasks, and we argue that properly addressing this challenge requires understanding this variation--dependency among tasks. To explicitly model task dependency, we consider nonlinear regression tasks and propose the assumption that these tasks are dependent in such a way that the data of the current task is a nonlinear transformation of previous data. With this model and under natural assumptions, we prove statistical recovery guarantees (more specifically, bounds on estimation errors) for several CL paradigms in practical use, including experience replay with data-independent regularization and data-independent weights that balance the losses of tasks, replay with data-dependent weights, and continual learning with data-dependent regularization (e.g., knowledge distillation). To the best of our knowledge, our bounds are informative in cases where prior work gives vacuous bounds.

</details>


### [82] [How Much Data is Enough? The Zeta Law of Discoverability in Biomedical Data, featuring the enigmatic Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17581)
*Paul M. Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于数据协方差算子谱结构的跨模态可发现性标度律框架，将性能指标表达为可识别谱模式上累积的信噪比能量，推导出由黎曼ζ函数主导的标度律


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生物医学数据集规模扩大和AI模型容量增长，需要理论指导来预测何时增加数据能显著改善性能，解决当前依赖经验标度曲线而缺乏理论基础的局限性

Method: 基于数据协方差算子的谱结构、任务对齐信号投影和学习表示，将AUC等性能指标表达为编码器和跨模态算子可识别谱模式上的累积信噪比能量，推导出由协方差谱和信号能量的幂律衰减主导的ζ类标度律

Result: 框架预测了交叉机制：简单模型在小样本量下表现最佳，而一旦有足够数据稳定额外自由度，高容量或多模态编码器表现更优；稀疏模型、低秩嵌入和多模态对比目标通过将有用信号集中到早期稳定模式来提高样本效率

Conclusion: 提出的ζ定律为预测何时扩展数据、改进表示或添加模态最可能加速科学发现提供了原则性方法，应用于多模态疾病分类、影像遗传学、功能MRI和拓扑数据分析

Abstract: How much data is enough to make a scientific discovery? As biomedical datasets scale to millions of samples and AI models grow in capacity, progress increasingly depends on predicting when additional data will substantially improve performance. In practice, model development often relies on empirical scaling curves measured across architectures, modalities, and dataset sizes, with limited theoretical guidance on when performance should improve, saturate, or exhibit cross-over behavior.
  We propose a scaling-law framework for cross-modal discoverability based on spectral structure of data covariance operators, task-aligned signal projections, and learned representations. Many performance metrics, including AUC, can be expressed in terms of cumulative signal-to-noise energy accumulated across identifiable spectral modes of an encoder and cross-modal operator. Under mild assumptions, this accumulation follows a zeta-like scaling law governed by power-law decay of covariance spectra and aligned signal energy, leading naturally to the appearance of the Riemann zeta function. Representation learning methods such as sparse models, low-rank embeddings, and multimodal contrastive objectives improve sample efficiency by concentrating useful signal into earlier stable modes, effectively steepening spectral decay and shifting scaling curves.
  The framework predicts cross-over regimes in which simpler models perform best at small sample sizes, while higher-capacity or multimodal encoders outperform them once sufficient data stabilizes additional degrees of freedom. Applications include multimodal disease classification, imaging genetics, functional MRI, and topological data analysis. The resulting zeta law provides a principled way to anticipate when scaling data, improving representations, or adding modalities is most likely to accelerate discovery.

</details>


### [83] [STEP-PD: Stage-Aware and Explainable Parkinson's Disease Severity Classification Using Multimodal Clinical Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17611)
*Md Mezbahul Islam,John Michael Templeton,Christian Poellabauer,Ananda Mohan Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STEP-PD：一个基于机器学习的帕金森病严重程度分期框架，利用PPMI队列的多模态临床数据，通过XGBoost实现高精度的三阶段严重程度分类，并提供了可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病是一种进展性疾病，症状负担和功能障碍随时间演变，严重程度分期对临床监测和治疗规划至关重要。然而，许多计算研究侧重于二元PD检测，未能充分利用重复随访临床评估进行阶段感知预测。

Method: 提出STEP-PD框架，利用帕金森病进展标志物倡议（PPMI）的所有可用访视数据，整合常规收集的主观问卷和客观临床评估指标。疾病严重程度使用Hoehn和Yahr分期定义，分为三个临床有意义的类别：健康、轻度PD（1-2期）和中重度PD（3-5期）。评估了三个二元分类问题和一个三分类严重程度任务，使用分层交叉验证和不平衡感知训练。为增强可解释性，使用SHAP提供全局解释和局部患者级瀑布图解释。

Result: XGBoost在所有任务中表现出最强和最稳定的性能：健康vs.轻度准确率95.48%，健康vs.中重度准确率99.44%，轻度vs.中重度准确率96.78%，三分类严重程度分类准确率94.14%，Macro-F1为0.8775。可解释性结果显示从早期运动特征向进展相关的轴向和平衡障碍转变。

Conclusion: PPMI队列内的多模态临床评估可以支持准确且可解释的访视级PD严重程度分层，为临床监测和治疗规划提供了有价值的工具。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is a progressive disorder in which symptom burden and functional impairment evolve over time, making severity staging essential for clinical monitoring and treatment planning. However, many computational studies emphasize binary PD detection and do not fully use repeated follow-up clinical assessments for stage-aware prediction. This study proposes STEP-PD, a severity-aware machine learning framework to classify PD severity using clinically interpretable boundaries. It leverages all available visits from the Parkinson's Progression Markers Initiative (PPMI) and integrates routinely collected subjective questionnaires and objective clinician-assessed measures. Disease severity is defined using Hoehn and Yahr staging and grouped into three clinically meaningful categories: Healthy, Mild PD (stages 1-2), and Moderate-to-Severe PD (stages 3-5). Three binary classification problems and a three-class severity task were evaluated using stratified cross-validation with imbalance-aware training. To enhance interpretability, SHAP was used to provide global explanations and local patient-level waterfall explanations. Across all tasks, XGBoost achieved the strongest and most stable performance, with accuracies of 95.48% (Healthy vs. Mild), 99.44% (Healthy vs. Moderate-to-Severe), and 96.78% (Mild vs. Moderate-to-Severe), and 94.14% accuracy with 0.8775 Macro-F1 for three-class severity classification. Explainability results highlight a shift from early motor features to progression-related axial and balance impairments. These findings show that multimodal clinical assessments within the PPMI cohort can support accurate and interpretable visit-level PD severity stratification.

</details>


### [84] [Conditional Attribution for Root Cause Analysis in Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17616)
*Shashank Mishra,Karan Patil,Cedric Schockaert,Didier Stricker,Jason Rambach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出条件归因框架，通过检索上下文相似正常状态来解释时间序列异常，避免不现实特征扰动，提高根因分析可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测的根因分析方法存在两个主要问题：1) 依赖不现实的特征扰动；2) 忽略时间和跨特征依赖关系，导致归因结果不可靠。需要一种能保持依赖关系并提供操作上有意义的解释方法。

Method: 提出条件归因框架，基于异常观测检索上下文相似的代表性正常实例作为基线。使用变分自编码器潜在空间和UMAP流形嵌入在低维表示中进行上下文检索，避免分布外伪影。引入置信感知和时间评估指标来评估解释可靠性。

Result: 在SWaT和MSDS基准测试上的实验表明，该方法能持续提高根因识别准确率、时间定位精度和鲁棒性，适用于多种异常检测模型。

Conclusion: 条件归因框架为复杂时间序列系统中的可解释异常诊断提供了实用工具，通过基于系统学习流形的上下文检索，实现了依赖保持且操作上有意义的解释。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) for time-series anomaly detection is critical for the reliable operation of complex real-world systems. Existing explanation methods often rely on unrealistic feature perturbations and ignore temporal and cross-feature dependencies, leading to unreliable attributions. We propose a conditional attribution framework that explains anomalies relative to contextually similar normal system states. Instead of using marginal or randomly sampled baselines, our method retrieves representative normal instances conditioned on the anomalous observation, enabling dependency-preserving and operationally meaningful explanations. To support high-dimensional time-series data, contextual retrieval is performed in learned low-dimensional representations using both variational autoencoder latent spaces and UMAP manifold embeddings. By grounding the retrieval process in the system's learned manifold, this strategy avoids out-of-distribution artifacts and ensures attribution fidelity while maintaining computational efficiency. We further introduce confidence-aware and temporal evaluation metrics for assessing explanation reliability and responsiveness. Experiments on the SWaT and MSDS benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently improves root-cause identification accuracy, temporal localization, and robustness across multiple anomaly detection models. These results highlight the practical utility of conditional attribution for explainable anomaly diagnosis in complex time-series systems. Code and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [85] [STRIKE: Additive Feature-Group-Aware Stacking Framework for Credit Default Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17622)
*Swattik Maiti,Ritik Pratap Singh,Fardina Fathmiul Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STRIKE是一种面向结构化表格信用风险数据的特征分组感知堆叠框架，通过将特征空间划分为语义连贯的组并在每组内训练独立学习器，然后通过元学习器聚合信号，在多个真实数据集上优于传统树模型和常规堆叠方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代信用数据集具有高维、异构和噪声特点，增加了单一模型过拟合风险并降低了分布变化下的鲁棒性。传统机器学习模型虽然可解释性强且经验性能好，但在处理复杂信用数据时面临挑战。

Method: STRIKE框架将特征空间划分为语义连贯的组，在每个特征组内训练独立学习器，然后通过元学习器聚合各组的预测结果。这种方法基于风险建模的加性视角，不同特征源提供互补证据，通过结构化聚合进行组合。

Result: 在三个真实数据集（企业破产和消费者贷款场景）上，STRIKE在AUC-ROC指标上始终优于强大的树基基准模型和常规堆叠方法。消融研究证实性能提升源于有意义的特征分解而非模型复杂度增加。

Conclusion: STRIKE是一个稳定、可扩展且可解释的信用风险违约预测框架，通过特征分组和结构化聚合提高了预测性能，为高维异构信用数据提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Credit risk default prediction remains a cornerstone of risk management in the financial industry. The task involves estimating the likelihood that a borrower will fail to meet debt obligations, an objective critical for lending decisions, portfolio optimization, and regulatory compliance. Traditional machine learning models such as logistic regression and tree-based ensembles are widely adopted for their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, modern credit datasets are high-dimensional, heterogeneous, and noisy, increasing overfitting risk in monolithic models and reducing robustness under distributional shift. We introduce STRIKE (Stacking via Targeted Representations of Isolated Knowledge Extractors), a feature-group-aware stacking framework for structured tabular credit risk data. Rather than training a single monolithic model on the complete dataset, STRIKE partitions the feature space into semantically coherent groups and trains independent learners within each group. This decomposition is motivated by an additive perspective on risk modeling, where distinct feature sources contribute complementary evidence that can be combined through a structured aggregation. The resulting group-specific predictions are integrated through a meta-learner that aggregates signals while maintaining robustness and modularity. We evaluate STRIKE on three real-world datasets spanning corporate bankruptcy and consumer lending scenarios. Across all settings, STRIKE consistently outperforms strong tree-based baselines and conventional stacking approaches in terms of AUC-ROC. Ablation studies confirm that performance gains stem from meaningful feature decomposition rather than increased model complexity. Our findings demonstrate that STRIKE is a stable, scalable, and interpretable framework for credit risk default prediction tasks.

</details>


### [86] [SLO-Guard: Crash-Aware, Budget-Consistent Autotuning for SLO-Constrained LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17627)
*Christian Lysenstøen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLO-Guard是一个针对vLLM大语言模型服务的崩溃感知自动调优器，将崩溃视为一等观察对象，通过两阶段方法（可行优先探索+热启动利用）在固定调优预算内更可预测地找到满足延迟SLO的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型服务配置调优面临挑战：许多配置会直接崩溃或无法满足用户可见的延迟目标，而标准黑盒优化器将这些失败视为浪费的试验。需要一种能够将崩溃作为一等观察对象处理的自动调优方法。

Method: SLO-Guard采用两阶段方法：1) 可行优先的热预算退火(TBA)探索阶段；2) 热启动的树结构Parzen估计器(TPE)利用阶段。还包括配置修复过程、GPU感知的KV缓存内存保护，以及四类崩溃分类法。

Result: 在Qwen2-1.5B模型和vLLM 0.19上的评估显示：SLO-Guard和均匀随机搜索都能达到75/75可行性且零崩溃，最佳延迟性能统计上持平。SLO-Guard的优势在于预算一致性：在快速服务机制中有更多试验(10.20 vs 7.40)，后切换一致性更高(0.876 vs 0.539)，最佳延迟的跨种子标准差比随机搜索紧4.4倍(2.26 ms vs 10.00 ms)。

Conclusion: SLO-Guard的核心价值不是找到更好的最终配置，而是在找到快速机制后，能够更可预测地使用固定调优预算，提供更高的配置调优一致性，这对于生产环境中的大语言模型服务部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Serving large language models under latency service-level objectives (SLOs) is a configuration-heavy systems problem with an unusually failure-prone search space: many plausible configurations crash outright or miss user-visible latency targets, and standard black-box optimizers treat these failures as wasted trials. We present SLO-Guard, a crash-aware autotuner for vLLM serving that treats crashes as first-class observations. SLO-Guard combines a feasible-first Thermal Budget Annealing (TBA) exploration phase with a warm-started Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) exploitation phase; the handoff replays all exploration history, including crashes encoded as extreme constraint violations. We additionally contribute a configuration-repair pass, a GPU-aware KV-cache memory guard, and a four-category crash taxonomy.
  We evaluate SLO-Guard on Qwen2-1.5B served with vLLM 0.19 on an NVIDIA A100 40GB. Across a pre-specified five-seed study, both SLO-Guard and uniform random search attain 75/75 feasibility with zero crashes under the corrected concurrent harness, and are statistically tied on best-achieved latency (Mann-Whitney two-sided p=0.84). SLO-Guard's advantage is in budget consistency: more trials in the fast-serving regime (10.20 vs. 7.40 out of 15; one-sided p=0.014) and higher post-handoff consistency (0.876 vs. 0.539; p=0.010). Under concurrent load, SLO-Guard's cross-seed standard deviation on best latency is 4.4x tighter than random search's (2.26 ms vs. 10.00 ms). A harness-replication analysis shows that the consistency findings survive an independent sequential-dispatch measurement condition.
  The central claim is not that SLO-Guard finds a better final configuration, but that it spends a fixed tuning budget more predictably once the fast regime has been found.

</details>


### [87] [Prior-Fitted Functional Flow: In-Context Generative Models for Pharmacokinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17670)
*César Ojeda,Niklas Hartung,Wilhelm Huisinga,Tim Jahn,Purity Kamene Kavwele,Marian Klose,Piyush Kumar,Ramsés J. Sánchez,Darius A. Faroughy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prior-Fitted Functional Flows：一种用于药代动力学的生成基础模型，无需手动参数调整即可实现零样本群体合成和个体预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决药代动力学中传统方法需要手动参数调整、难以处理稀疏不规则数据、缺乏不确定性校准的问题，实现零样本的群体合成和个体轨迹预测

Method: 学习显式条件于整个研究群体稀疏不规则数据的功能向量场，构建新的开放获取文献语料库来指导先验分布

Result: 在广泛的实际数据集上展示了最先进的预测准确性，能够生成一致的虚拟队列并对部分观测的患者轨迹进行具有校准不确定性的预测

Conclusion: Prior-Fitted Functional Flows 是一种有效的药代动力学生成基础模型，能够实现零样本的群体合成和个体预测，具有校准的不确定性估计

Abstract: We introduce Prior-Fitted Functional Flows, a generative foundation model for pharmacokinetics that enables zero-shot population synthesis and individual forecasting without manual parameter tuning. We learn functional vector fields, explicitly conditioned on the sparse, irregular data of an entire study population. This enables the generation of coherent virtual cohorts as well as forecasting of partially observed patient trajectories with calibrated uncertainty. We construct a new open-access literature corpus to inform our priors, and demonstrate state-of-the-art predictive accuracy on extensive real-world datasets.

</details>


### [88] [Grokking of Diffusion Models: Case Study on Modular Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17673)
*Joon Hyeok Kim,Yong-Hyun Park,Mattis Dalsætra Østby,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散模型在流匹配目标训练下，在模加任务上表现出延迟泛化现象，研究揭示了其内部计算机制如何实现从连续像素空间生成到离散符号推理的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型在经验上取得了成功，但其泛化机制从计算角度仍不明确。本文旨在通过可控分析揭示扩散模型内部的计算机制，特别是它们如何将连续像素空间生成与离散符号推理联系起来。

Method: 使用流匹配目标训练扩散模型，在模加任务上研究延迟泛化现象。在两个数据机制下进行分析：单图像机制下通过机制解剖研究模型实现；多样图像机制下研究模型如何利用迭代采样过程将任务分解为算术计算和视觉去噪两个阶段。

Result: 在单图像机制下，模型通过组合单个操作数的周期性表示来实现模加运算。在多样图像机制下，模型利用迭代采样过程将任务划分为算术计算阶段和视觉去噪阶段，这两个阶段由一个关键时间步阈值分隔。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了扩散模型中算法学习的机制分解，揭示了这些模型如何桥接连续像素空间生成和离散符号推理。研究结果表明扩散模型能够通过内部计算机制实现复杂的符号推理任务。

Abstract: Despite their empirical success, how diffusion models generalize remains poorly understood from a mechanistic perspective. We demonstrate that diffusion models trained with flow-matching objectives exhibit grokking--delayed generalization after overfitting--on modular addition, enabling controlled analysis of their internal computations. We study this phenomenon across two levels of data regime. In a single-image regime, mechanistic dissection reveals that the model implements modular addition by composing periodic representations of individual operands. In a diverse-image regime with high intraclass variability, we find that the model leverages its iterative sampling process to partition the task into an arithmetic computation phase followed by a visual denoising phase, separated by a critical timestep threshold. Our work provides the mechanistic decomposition of algorithmic learning in diffusion models, revealing how these models bridge continuous pixel-space generation and discrete symbolic reasoning.

</details>


### [89] [SafeAnchor: Preventing Cumulative Safety Erosion in Continual Domain Adaptation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17691)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeAnchor框架通过识别LoRA参数空间中的低秩安全子空间，约束领域特定梯度更新到这些子空间的正交补空间，并监控安全漂移，在持续多领域适应中保持大语言模型的安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的安全对齐很脆弱，主要集中在输出前几个token，且通过少量对抗样本即可逆转。在现实部署中，模型需要在医学、法律、代码等多个领域进行顺序适应，导致安全护栏逐渐被侵蚀。现有安全保护方法仅针对单任务微调，无法解决多领域顺序设置的问题。

Method: SafeAnchor框架包含三个步骤：1) 通过Fisher信息特征分解识别LoRA参数空间中的低秩安全子空间；2) 将领域特定梯度更新约束到这些安全子空间的正交补空间；3) 通过阈值触发的纠正回放监控残余安全漂移。

Result: 在Llama-2-7B-Chat和Mistral-7B-Instruct模型上，通过三领域流程和八个基准测试，SafeAnchor保持了原始安全对齐的93.2%，比所有基线方法高出18-42个百分点，同时在领域任务上与非约束微调的差距在1.5个百分点以内。

Conclusion: SafeAnchor是首个针对多领域顺序适应场景的安全保护框架，通过锚定安全子空间、约束梯度更新和监控漂移，有效解决了大语言模型在持续适应过程中的安全对齐退化问题。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models is remarkably shallow: it is concentrated in the first few output tokens and reversible by fine-tuning on as few as 100 adversarial examples. This fragility becomes critical in real-world deployment, where models undergo sequential adaptation across domains such as medicine, law, and code, causing safety guardrails to erode cumulatively. Yet all existing safety-preserving methods target only single-task fine-tuning, leaving the multi-domain sequential setting entirely unaddressed.
  We introduce SafeAnchor, a framework that anchors safety in place throughout continual adaptation. SafeAnchor first identifies low-rank safety subspaces in LoRA parameter space via Fisher Information eigendecomposition, then constrains domain-specific gradient updates to the orthogonal complement of these subspaces, and finally monitors for residual safety drift with threshold-triggered corrective replay. Evaluated on Llama-2-7B-Chat and Mistral-7B-Instruct across a three-domain pipeline and eight benchmarks, SafeAnchor retains 93.2% of original safety alignment, outperforming all baselines by 18-42 points, while matching unconstrained fine-tuning to within 1.5 points on domain tasks.

</details>


### [90] [CAPO: Counterfactual Credit Assignment in Sequential Cooperative Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17693)
*Shripad Deshmukh,Jayakumar Subramanian,Raghavendra Addanki,Nikos Vlassis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SeqAU（顺序贵族效用）作为顺序合作团队中个体贡献的独特学习信号，并基于此开发了CAPO算法，无需额外环境交互即可计算个体优势


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在顺序合作团队中，当智能体按固定顺序行动且共享团队奖励时，很难评估每个智能体的贡献度。特别是当智能体逐个更新时，早期收集的数据不再反映新策略，导致学习困难

Method: 引入SeqAU（Sequential Aristocrat Utility）作为最大化每个智能体动作个体可学习性的独特信号，基于此推导出CAPO（Counterfactual Advantage Policy Optimization）算法。CAPO从团队奖励中拟合每个智能体的奖励分解，并通过当前策略的少量前向传播计算个体优势，无需额外环境调用

Result: 提供了理论上的偏差和方差界限，并在受控的顺序赌博机环境中验证了这些界限。CAPO相对于标准基线的优势随着团队规模的增大而增长。该框架具有通用性，多LLM管道是其自然应用场景

Conclusion: SeqAU为顺序合作团队提供了有效的个体贡献评估框架，CAPO算法通过无需额外环境交互的优势计算，解决了顺序团队强化学习中的信用分配问题，特别适用于大规模团队和多LLM管道等应用场景

Abstract: In cooperative teams where agents act in a fixed order and share a single team reward, it is hard to know how much each agent contributed, and harder still when agents are updated one at a time because data collected earlier no longer reflects the new policies. We introduce the Sequential Aristocrat Utility (SeqAU), the unique per-agent learning signal that maximizes the individual learnability of each agent's action, extending the classical framework of Wolpert and Tumer (2002) to this sequential setting. From SeqAU we derive CAPO (Counterfactual Advantage Policy Optimization), a critic-free policy-gradient algorithm. CAPO fits a per-agent reward decomposition from group rewards and computes the per-agent advantage in closed form plus a handful of forward passes through the current policy, requiring no extra environment calls beyond the initial batch. We give analytic bias and variance bounds and validate them on a controlled sequential bandit, where CAPO's advantage over standard baselines grows with the team size. The framework is general; multi-LLM pipelines are a natural deployment target.

</details>


### [91] [MoE-nD: Per-Layer Mixture-of-Experts Routing for Multi-Axis KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17695)
*Libo Sun,Peixiong He,Po-Wei Harn,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE-nD：一种混合专家框架，通过为每个Transformer层分配不同的KV缓存压缩策略（逐出率和量化位数），在全局内存预算下实现异构压缩，显著提升长上下文推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存内存是长上下文LLM推理的主要瓶颈。现有压缩方法对四维KV张量（序列、精度、头维度、跨层共享）的单一维度进行压缩，且对所有层采用相同策略。研究发现不同层对压缩操作的响应差异很大，均匀压缩策略会损失精度。

Method: 提出MoE-nD混合专家框架：1）为每个层路由到特定的（逐出率、K位数、V位数）三元组；2）离线校准的贪婪求解器在全局内存预算下选择最小化预测质量损失的路由；3）推理时通过单一注意力补丁联合应用每层异构逐出和量化。

Result: 在LongBench-v1 4任务子集（16k输入）上，MoE-nD异构变体在14倍压缩（136MB）下匹配未压缩基线（1.9GB），而其他压缩基线在可比或更小内存下得分低于8/100。在AIME推理基准上，比最强每层量化基线提升6-27分。MATH-500和LongBench TREC任务无提升，原因是输入较短，求解器选择大部分层保持率为1.0。

Conclusion: 不同Transformer层对KV缓存压缩的敏感性存在显著差异，异构压缩策略相比均匀压缩能实现更好的精度-内存权衡。MoE-nD框架通过为每层选择最优压缩组合，在长上下文任务中实现显著性能提升，同时为短输入场景提供了清晰的失效边界。

Abstract: KV cache memory is the dominant bottleneck for long-context LLM inference. Existing compression methods each act on a single axis of the four-dimensional KV tensor -- token eviction (sequence), quantization (precision), low-rank projection (head dimension), or cross-layer sharing -- but apply the same recipe to every layer. We show that this homogeneity leaves accuracy on the table: different layers respond very differently to each compression operation, and the optimal per-layer mix of eviction and quantization is far from uniform. We propose MoE-nD, a mixture-of-experts framework that routes each layer to its own (eviction-ratio, K-bits, V-bits) tuple under a global memory budget. An offline-calibrated greedy solver chooses the routing that minimizes predicted quality loss; at inference time, per-layer heterogeneous eviction and quantization are applied jointly through a single attention patch. On a 4-task subset of LongBench-v1 (16k inputs, n=50 per task, adapted reasoning-model protocol; see section Experiments), MoE-nD's hetero variant matches our uncompressed 1.9~GB baseline at 14x compression (136~MB) while every other compressed baseline we tested (1d, 2d_uniform, 2d) at comparable or smaller memory stays under 8/100. The gains hold on AIME reasoning benchmarks (+6 to +27 pts over the strongest per-layer-quantization baseline across eight configurations). Two null results -- MATH-500 and LongBench's TREC -- share a principled cause (short inputs, solver picks keep=1.0 on most layers), cleanly characterizing when per-layer eviction routing has headroom to help.

</details>


### [92] [The Geometric Canary: Predicting Steerability and Detecting Drift via Representational Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17698)
*Prashant C. Raju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出几何稳定性作为语言模型部署的两个关键问题的统一解决方案：预测模型是否接受定向行为控制，以及检测其内部结构何时退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可靠部署需要解决两个看似不同但具有共同几何基础的问题：预测模型是否会接受定向行为控制（线性可操控性），以及检测模型内部结构何时退化。当前缺乏统一的框架来同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出几何稳定性概念，即表示之间成对距离结构的一致性。开发了两种变体：监督Shesha变体测量任务对齐的几何稳定性，用于预测线性可操控性；无监督稳定性用于检测表示漂移。在35-69个嵌入模型和三个NLP任务上进行评估。

Result: 监督稳定性在预测线性可操控性方面达到近乎完美的准确率（ρ=0.89-0.97），且能捕捉类别可分性之外的独特方差（偏ρ=0.62-0.76）。无监督稳定性在漂移检测方面表现优异，测量到的几何变化是CKA的2倍（Llama中达5.23倍），在73%的模型中提供更早预警，且误报率比Procrustes低6倍。

Conclusion: 监督和无监督几何稳定性形成了LLM部署生命周期的互补诊断工具：前者用于部署前的可操控性评估，后者用于部署后的监控。任务对齐对于可操控性预测至关重要，而无监督稳定性在漂移检测方面表现卓越。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of language models requires two capabilities that appear distinct but share a common geometric foundation: predicting whether a model will accept targeted behavioral control, and detecting when its internal structure degrades. We show that geometric stability, the consistency of a representation's pairwise distance structure, addresses both. Supervised Shesha variants that measure task-aligned geometric stability predict linear steerability with near-perfect accuracy ($ρ= 0.89$-$0.97$) across 35-69 embedding models and three NLP tasks, capturing unique variance beyond class separability (partial $ρ= 0.62$-$0.76$). A critical dissociation emerges: unsupervised stability fails entirely for steering on real-world tasks ($ρ\approx 0.10$), revealing that task alignment is essential for controllability prediction. However, unsupervised stability excels at drift detection, measuring nearly $2\times$ greater geometric change than CKA during post-training alignment (up to $5.23\times$ in Llama) while providing earlier warning in 73\% of models and maintaining a $6\times$ lower false alarm rate than Procrustes. Together, supervised and unsupervised stability form complementary diagnostics for the LLM deployment lifecycle: one for pre-deployment controllability assessment, the other for post-deployment monitoring.

</details>


### [93] [Modeling Higher-Order Brain Interactions via a Multi-View Information Bottleneck Framework for fMRI-based Psychiatric Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17713)
*Kunyu Zhang,Qiang Li,Vince D. Calhoun,Shujian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于O信息的多视图信息瓶颈框架，用于fMRI精神病学诊断，通过融合二阶、三阶和四阶高阶交互，显著提升了诊断性能并揭示了可解释的协同-冗余模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有fMRI精神病诊断方法主要依赖成对脑连接，忽略了高阶交互作用，而超图方法虽然能编码高阶交互，但通常基于启发式相似度度量构建，且不能明确区分交互是协同主导还是冗余主导。

Method: 引入O信息作为有符号的高阶交互信息度量，开发了高斯解析近似和随机化矩阵Rényi熵估计两种加速策略，构建了融合成对、三元和四元脑交互的三视图信息瓶颈框架，明确惩罚冗余信息。

Result: 在四个基准数据集（REST-meta-MDD、ABIDE、UCLA、ADNI）上评估，相比11种基线方法（包括最先进的GNN和超图方法）取得了一致性改进，计算速度提升超过30倍，并揭示了可解释的区域级协同-冗余模式。

Conclusion: 提出的多视图信息瓶颈框架通过系统融合不同阶次的高阶交互，显著提升了fMRI精神病诊断性能，同时提供了传统超图方法无法明确表征的协同-冗余模式的可解释性分析。

Abstract: Resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) has emerged as a cornerstone for psychiatric diagnosis, yet most approaches rely on pairwise brain cortical or sub-cortical connectivities that overlooks higher-order interactions (HOIs) central to complex brain dynamics. While hypergraph methods encode HOIs through predefined hyperedges, their construction typically relies on heuristic similarity metrics and does not explicitly characterize whether interactions are synergy- or redundancy-dominated. In this paper, we introduce $O$-information, a signed measure that characterizes the informational nature of HOIs, and integrate third- and fourth-order $O$-information into a unified multi-view information bottleneck framework for fMRI-based psychiatric diagnosis. To enable scalable $O$-information estimation, we further develop two independent acceleration strategies: a Gaussian analytical approximation and a randomized matrix-based Rényi entropy estimator, achieving over a 30-fold computational speedup compared with conventional estimators. Our tri-view architecture systematically fuses pairwise, triadic, and tetradic brain interactions, capturing comprehensive brain connectivity while explicitly penalizing redundancy. Extensive evaluation across four benchmark datasets (REST-meta-MDD, ABIDE, UCLA, ADNI) demonstrates consistent improvements, outperforming 11 baseline methods including state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN) and hypergraph based approaches. Moreover, our method reveals interpretable region-level synergy-redundancy patterns which are not explicitly characterized by conventional hypergraph formulations.

</details>


### [94] [Efficient Federated RLHF via Zeroth-Order Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17747)
*Deyi Wang,Qining Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于资源受限边缘设备的联邦RLHF算法Par-S²ZPO，基于零阶优化和二进制扰动，在通信、计算和内存方面高效，理论证明其样本复杂度与集中式相当但策略更新更快，实验在MuJoCo任务上优于FedAvg-based RLHF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决资源受限边缘设备（如边缘设备）在联邦学习设置下进行人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）的挑战，需要设计通信、计算和内存高效的算法。

Method: 提出Par-S²ZPO算法，基于零阶优化和二进制扰动，通过分区、基于符号的随机零阶策略优化实现低通信、计算和内存复杂度。

Result: 理论分析建立了Par-S²ZPO收敛率上界，显示其样本复杂度与集中式相当但策略更新迭代更快；实验在四个MuJoCo RL任务上优于基于FedAvg的RLHF方法。

Conclusion: Par-S²ZPO是一种高效的联邦RLHF算法，适用于资源受限边缘设备，在保持与集中式相当样本复杂度的同时实现更快的策略收敛，并在实际任务中表现优异。

Abstract: This paper considers reinforcement learning from human feedback in a federated learning setting with resource-constrained agents, such as edge devices. We propose an efficient federated RLHF algorithm, named Partitioned, Sign-based Stochastic Zeroth-order Policy Optimization (Par-S$^2$ZPO). The algorithm is built on zeroth-order optimization with binary perturbation, resulting in low communication, computation, and memory complexity by design. Our theoretical analysis establishes an upper bound on the convergence rate of Par-S$^2$ZPO, revealing that it is as efficient as its centralized counterpart in terms of sample complexity but converges faster in terms of policy update iterations. Our experimental results show that it outperforms a FedAvg-based RLHF on four MuJoCo RL tasks.

</details>


### [95] [HiP-LoRA: Budgeted Spectral Plasticity for Robust Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17751)
*Lixian Chen,Jianhong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiP-LoRA：一种频谱感知的参数高效微调框架，通过将更新分解为主通道和残差通道，解决LoRA中的频谱干扰问题，在保持预训练能力的同时提升任务适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA作为参数高效微调的标准模块化解决方案，存在频谱干扰问题：低秩更新往往集中在预训练权重的主导奇异方向上，这会扰动模型的通用能力，导致灾难性遗忘和脆弱的多适配器合并。需要一种频谱感知的适应框架来解决这些问题。

Method: HiP-LoRA利用预训练层的缓存奇异值分解（SVD），将更新分解为两个通道：1）主导奇异子空间内的主通道；2）正交补空间中的残差低秩通道。通过对主通道施加奇异值加权的稳定性预算，持续平衡预训练行为保持与任务特定可塑性。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B上的实验表明，在匹配预算下，HiP-LoRA显著减少了预训练能力退化，降低了多适配器合并失败率，在干扰敏感任务（如持续调优和知识编辑）中稳健地优于基线方法。

Conclusion: HiP-LoRA通过频谱感知的适应框架有效解决了LoRA的频谱干扰问题，在保持预训练模型通用能力的同时实现了更好的任务适应性和多适配器合并鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adapting foundation models under resource budgets relies heavily on Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), with LoRA being a standard modular solution. However, LoRA suffers from spectral interference. Low-rank updates often concentrate energy on the leading singular directions of pretrained weights, perturbing general capabilities and causing catastrophic forgetting and fragile multi-adapter merging. To resolve this, we propose HiP-LoRA, a spectrum-aware adaptation framework. Utilizing the cached singular value decomposition (SVD) of pretrained layers, HiP-LoRA decomposes updates into two channels: a principal channel within the dominant singular subspace, and a residual low-rank channel in the orthogonal complement. A singular-value-weighted stability budget on the principal channel continuously balances pretrained behavior preservation with task-specific plasticity. Experiments on Llama-3.1-8B demonstrate that under matched budgets, HiP-LoRA drastically reduces pretraining degradation and multi-adapter MergeFail, robustly outperforming baselines in interference-sensitive tasks like continual tuning and knowledge editing.

</details>


### [96] [LLM-AUG: Robust Wireless Data Augmentation with In-Context Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17770)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Manan Tiwari,Sayanta Seth,Vijay K. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM-AUG：利用大语言模型上下文学习在嵌入空间生成合成训练样本的数据增强框架，显著提升无线通信任务在低样本量下的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信领域深度学习面临数据稀缺瓶颈，收集标记RF数据成本高、耗时长或受操作限制。传统生成方法需要训练特定任务模型，难以快速适应低样本场景。

Method: 提出LLM-AUG框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，通过结构化提示直接在学习的嵌入空间中生成合成训练样本，无需训练任务特定生成模型。

Result: 在调制分类和干扰分类任务上，LLM-AUG在低样本设置下持续优于传统增强和深度生成基线，仅用15%标记数据达到接近oracle性能。在分布偏移下表现出更强鲁棒性，相对扩散基线的相对增益在RadioML和IC数据集上分别达到67.6%和35.7%。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可作为无线机器学习中有效实用的数据增强器，在演进的无线环境中实现鲁棒且数据高效的学习。

Abstract: Data scarcity remains a fundamental bottleneck in applying deep learning to wireless communication problems, particularly in scenarios where collecting labeled Radio Frequency (RF) data is expensive, time-consuming, or operationally constrained. This paper proposes LLM-AUG, a data augmentation framework that leverages in-context learning in large language models (LLMs) to generate synthetic training samples directly in a learned embedding space. Unlike conventional generative approaches that require training task-specific models, LLM-AUG performs data generation through structured prompting, enabling rapid adaptation in low-shot regimes. We evaluate LLM-AUG on two representative tasks: modulation classification and interference classification using the RadioML 2016.10A dataset, and the Interference Classification (IC) dataset respectively. Results show that LLM-AUG consistently outperforms traditional augmentation and deep generative baselines across low-shot settings and reaches near oracle performance using only 15% labeled data. LLM-AUG further demonstrates improved robustness under distribution shifts, yielding a 29.4% relative gain over diffusion-based augmentation at a lower SNR value. On the RadioML and IC datasets, LLM-AUG yields a relative gain of 67.6% and 35.7% over the diffusion-based baseline. The t-SNE visualizations further validate that synthetic samples generated by better preserve class structure in the embedding space, leading to more consistent and informative augmentations. These results demonstrate that LLMs can serve as effective and practical data augmenters for wireless machine learning, enabling robust and data-efficient learning in evolving wireless environments.

</details>


### [97] [TeleEmbedBench: A Multi-Corpus Embedding Benchmark for RAG in Telecommunications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17778)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Vijay K Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TeleEmbedBench是首个针对电信领域的大规模多语料嵌入基准测试，包含O-RAN、3GPP和srsRAN三个异构语料库，通过自动化流水线生成9000个问题-语块对，评估显示LLM嵌入模型在检索准确性和鲁棒性方面显著优于传统句子转换器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信领域LLM部署日益增多，依赖RAG技术适应不断演进的标准，但现有通用基准测试无法评估电信语料库（密集、缩写多、高度交叉引用）的特性，缺乏专门针对电信领域的嵌入模型评估基准。

Method: 1) 构建TeleEmbedBench基准：包含O-RAN规范、3GPP发布文档和srsRAN开源代码库三个异构语料库；2) 自动化数据生成流水线：使用一个LLM从文本块生成特定查询，另一个LLM根据严格标准验证；3) 评估8个嵌入模型：包括标准句子转换器和LLM嵌入器；4) 引入TeleEmbedBench-Clean评估模型对噪声、不完整用户查询的鲁棒性。

Result: 1) LLM嵌入模型（如Qwen3和EmbeddingGemma）在检索准确性和鲁棒性方面显著优于传统句子转换器；2) 领域特定任务指令对原始源代码嵌入有改进，但对自然语言电信规范的检索性能反而下降；3) 自动化流水线成功生成了9000个问题-语块对，跨越三个标准语块大小（512、1024、2048 tokens）。

Conclusion: TeleEmbedBench填补了电信领域嵌入模型评估的空白，证明了LLM嵌入模型在电信RAG系统中的优越性，揭示了领域特定指令对不同类型电信内容的不同影响，为电信领域LLM部署提供了重要基准和指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in the telecommunications domain for critical tasks, relying heavily on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to adapt general-purpose models to continuously evolving standards. However, a significant gap exists in evaluating the embedding models that power these RAG pipelines, as general-purpose benchmarks fail to capture the dense, acronym-heavy, and highly cross-referential nature of telecommunications corpora. To address this, we introduce TeleEmbedBench, the first large-scale, multi-corpus embedding benchmark designed specifically for telecommunications. The benchmark spans three heterogeneous corpora: O-RAN Alliance specifications, 3GPP release documents, and the srsRAN open-source codebase, comprising 9,000 question-chunk pairs across three standard chunk sizes (512, 1024, and 2048 tokens). To construct this dataset at scale without manual annotation bottlenecks, we employ a novel automated pipeline where one LLM generates specific queries from text chunks and a secondary LLM validates them across strict criteria. We comprehensively evaluate eight embedding models, spanning standard sentence-transformers and LLM-based embedders. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based embedders, such as Qwen3 and EmbeddingGemma, consistently and significantly outperform traditional sentence-transformers in both retrieval accuracy and robustness against cross-domain interference. Additionally, we introduce TeleEmbedBench-Clean to evaluate model robustness against noisy, incomplete user queries. Finally, our analysis reveals that while domain-specific task instructions improve embedder performance for raw source code, they paradoxically degrade retrieval performance for natural language telecommunications specifications.

</details>


### [98] [Ranking Abuse via Strategic Pairwise Data Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17805)
*Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MLE-based排名系统对对抗性扰动存在脆弱性，ASSA攻击方法能有效识别高影响力扰动，少量策略性投票者即可显著改变全局排名


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最大似然估计的成对排名系统（如Bradley-Terry模型）广泛用于聚合成对比较偏好，但其在策略性数据操纵下的鲁棒性尚未得到充分理解

Method: 将操纵任务建模为约束组合优化问题，提出自适应子集选择攻击（ASSA）来高效识别高影响力扰动，在合成数据和真实世界选举数据集上进行实验

Result: MLE-based排名表现出尖锐的相变行为：超过小扰动预算后，有限数量的策略性投票者能显著改变全局排名；ASSA方法在约束预算下始终优于随机和贪婪基线

Conclusion: MLE-based排名机制对结构化扰动具有基本敏感性，集体决策系统需要更鲁棒的聚合方法

Abstract: Pairwise ranking systems based on Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE), such as the Bradley-Terry model, are widely used to aggregate preferences from pairwise comparisons. However, their robustness under strategic data manipulation remains insufficiently understood.
  In this paper, we study the vulnerability of MLE-based ranking systems to adversarial perturbations. We formulate the manipulation task as a constrained combinatorial optimization problem and propose an Adaptive Subset Selection Attack (ASSA) to efficiently identify high-impact perturbations.
  Experimental results on both synthetic data and real-world election datasets show that MLE-based rankings exhibit a sharp phase-transition behavior: beyond a small perturbation budget, a limited number of strategic voters can significantly alter the global ranking. In particular, our method consistently outperforms random and greedy baselines under constrained budgets.
  These findings reveal a fundamental sensitivity of MLE-based ranking mechanisms to structured perturbations and highlight the need for more robust aggregation methods in collective decision-making systems.

</details>


### [99] [Efficient Diffusion Models under Nonconvex Equality and Inequality constraints via Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17838)
*Kijung Jeon,Michael Muehlebach,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于非凸可行集约束扩散模型的统一框架，通过着陆机制替代投影，结合过阻尼和欠阻尼动力学，显著降低计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程应用中的生成建模需要在约束集内进行（如分子生成、机器人学），现有方法在非凸可行集上投影计算成本高且不稳定

Method: 提出统一框架处理等式和不等式约束，引入计算高效的着陆机制替代投影，结合过阻尼和欠阻尼动力学加速混合过程

Result: 在包含等式和混合约束的基准测试中，达到与最先进基线相当的样本质量，同时显著减少计算成本、函数评估和内存使用

Conclusion: 该方法为非凸可行集上的扩散模型提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，通过着陆机制和动力学优化有效平衡了约束满足与计算效率

Abstract: Generative modeling within constrained sets is essential for scientific and engineering applications involving physical, geometric, or safety requirements (e.g., molecular generation, robotics). We present a unified framework for constrained diffusion models on generic nonconvex feasible sets $Σ$ that simultaneously enforces equality and inequality constraints throughout the diffusion process. Our framework incorporates both overdamped and underdamped dynamics for forward and backward sampling. A key algorithmic innovation is a computationally efficient landing mechanism that replaces costly and often ill-defined projections onto $Σ$, ensuring feasibility without iterative Newton solves or projection failures. By leveraging underdamped dynamics, we accelerate mixing toward the prior distribution, effectively alleviating the high simulation costs typically associated with constrained diffusion. Empirically, this approach reduces function evaluations and memory usage during both training and inference while preserving sample quality. On benchmarks featuring equality and mixed constraints, our method achieves comparable sample quality to state-of-the-art baselines while significantly reducing computational cost, providing a practical and scalable solution for diffusion on nonconvex feasible sets.

</details>


### [100] [M100: An Orchestrated Dataflow Architecture Powering General AI Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17862)
*Yan Xie,Changkui Mao,Changsong Wu,Chao Lu,Chao Suo,Cheng Qian,Chun Yang,Danyang Zhu,Hengchang Xiong,Hongzhan Lu,Hongzhen Liu,Jiafu Liu,Jie Chen,Jie Dai,Junfeng Tang,Kai Liu,Kun Li,Lipeng Ge,Meng Sun,Min Luo,Peng Chen,Peng Wang,Shaodong Yang,Shibin Tang,Shibo Chen,Weikang Zhang,Xiao Ling,Xiaobo Du,Xin Wu,Yang Liu,Yi Jiang,Yihua Jin,Yin Huang,Yuli Zhang,Zhen Yuan,Zhiyuan Man,Zhongxiao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M100是理想汽车开发的面向自动驾驶、大语言模型和智能交互的AI推理架构，采用数据流并行设计和软硬件协同优化，以张量为基本数据单元，相比GPGPU架构在效率和成本上更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习AI技术的发展，对通用AI计算架构的需求日益增长。GPGPU架构虽然通用但效率不足，而特定领域架构(DSAs)又难以适应快速演进的AI应用场景。理想汽车需要为自动驾驶、大语言模型和智能交互等关键领域开发高效、经济且通用的AI推理架构。

Method: 采用数据流并行架构，通过编译器-架构协同设计优化计算和数据在时空维度的移动。以张量为基本数据单元，在编译器、固件和硬件层面选择合适的操作粒度进行调度、分发和执行。通过软硬件协同设计减少缓存依赖，让张量计算由编译器和运行时管理的数据流驱动，在计算单元和片上/片外存储器之间流动。

Result: M100在自动驾驶应用(如UniAD)和大语言模型(如LLaMA)等多样化推理任务中展现出通用AI计算能力。基准测试显示，M100在自动驾驶应用中比GPGPU架构表现更优，具有更高的利用率，代表了未来通用AI计算的有前景方向。

Conclusion: M100通过数据流并行架构和软硬件协同设计，实现了高效、经济且通用的AI推理计算架构，特别适用于自动驾驶、大语言模型和智能交互等关键领域，为未来通用AI计算提供了有前景的技术方向。

Abstract: As deep learning-based AI technologies gain momentum, the demand for general-purpose AI computing architectures continues to grow. While GPGPU-based architectures offer versatility for diverse AI workloads, they often fall short in efficiency and cost-effectiveness. Various Domain-Specific Architectures (DSAs) excel at particular AI tasks but struggle to extend across broader applications or adapt to the rapidly evolving AI landscape. M100 is Li Auto's response: a performant, cost-effective architecture for AI inference in Autonomous Driving (AD), Large Language Models (LLMs), and intelligent human interactions, domains crucial to today's most competitive automobile platforms. M100 employs a dataflow parallel architecture, where compiler-architecture co-design orchestrates not only computation but, more critically, data movement across time and space. Leveraging dataflow computing efficiency, our hardware-software co-design improves system performance while reducing hardware complexity and cost. M100 largely eliminates caching: tensor computations are driven by compiler- and runtime-managed data streams flowing between computing elements and on/off-chip memories, yielding greater efficiency and scalability than cache-based systems. Another key principle was selecting the right operational granularity for scheduling, issuing, and execution across compiler, firmware, and hardware. Recognizing commonalities in AI workloads, we chose the tensor as the fundamental data element. M100 demonstrates general AI computing capability across diverse inference applications, including UniAD (for AD) and LLaMA (for LLMs). Benchmarks show M100 outperforms GPGPU architectures in AD applications with higher utilization, representing a promising direction for future general AI computing.

</details>


### [101] [LEPO: \underline{L}atent R\underline{e}asoning \underline{P}olicy \underline{O}ptimization for Large Language~Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17892)
*Yuyan Zhou,Jiarui Yu,Hande Dong,Zhezheng Hao,Hong Wang,Jianqing Zhang,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEPO框架通过Gumbel-Softmax在潜在推理中注入可控随机性，将强化学习直接应用于连续潜在表示，显著提升了离散和潜在推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法缺乏随机采样，导致确定性推理崩溃，无法发现多样化的推理路径。需要恢复LLMs的探索能力并增强其与强化学习的兼容性。

Method: 提出LEPO框架：1) 使用Gumbel-Softmax在潜在推理中注入可控随机性；2) 在rollout阶段保持随机性以采样多样化轨迹；3) 在优化阶段构建统一的梯度估计，同时优化潜在表示和离散token。

Result: 大量实验表明，LEPO在离散和潜在推理任务上显著优于现有的强化学习方法。

Conclusion: LEPO通过将强化学习直接应用于连续潜在表示，成功解决了潜在推理中缺乏随机性的问题，为LLMs的推理能力提供了有效的增强框架。

Abstract: Recently, latent reasoning has been introduced into large language models (LLMs) to leverage rich information within a continuous space. However, without stochastic sampling, these methods inevitably collapse to deterministic inference, failing to discover diverse reasoning paths. To bridge the gap, we inject controllable stochasticity into latent reasoning via Gumbel-Softmax, restoring LLMs' exploratory capacity and enhancing their compatibility with Reinforcement Learning (RL). Building on this, we propose \textbf{\underline{L}}atent R\textbf{\underline{e}}asoning \textbf{\underline{P}}olicy \textbf{\underline{O}}ptimization~(\textbf{LEPO}), a novel framework that applies RL directly to continuous latent representations. Specifically, in rollout stage, LEPO maintains stochasticity to enable diverse trajectory sampling, while in optimization stage, LEPO constructs a unified gradient estimation for both latent representations and discrete tokens. Extensive experiments show that LEPO significantly outperforms existing RL methods for discrete and latent reasoning.

</details>


### [102] [Can Explicit Physical Feasibility Benefit VLA Learning? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17896)
*Yubai Wei,Chen Wu,Hashem Haghbayan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在视觉-语言-动作模型中引入显式可行性监督，通过几何约束提升机器人策略的物理可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型通过大规模模仿学习训练，但缺乏对硬物理约束（如避障、运动学可行性）的显式监督，导致物理可行行为的几何结构只能从演示中隐式推断

Method: 提出基于几何的可行性目标，将其整合到基于扩散的VLA策略训练阶段，以障碍感知操作为受控实验来系统评估几何依赖的物理可行性

Result: 实验表明，在VLA训练中增加可行性监督能同时提升物理可靠性和整体任务性能，并在低数据情况下增强学习效率

Conclusion: 显式可行性信号能有效补充基于模仿的VLA学习，为开发更可靠的VLA策略提供了潜力

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map multimodal inputs directly to robot actions and are typically trained through large-scale imitation learning. While this paradigm has shown strong performance, prevailing VLA training procedures do not explicitly supervise hard physical constraints such as obstacle avoidance or kinematic feasibility. As a result, the geometric structure underlying physically feasible behavior must be inferred only implicitly from demonstrations. In this paper, we study whether introducing explicit feasibility supervision can provide effective structured guidance for VLA policies. We formulate a simple geometry-grounded feasibility objective and integrate it into the training stage of a diffusion-based VLA policy. To evaluate this idea systematically, we use obstacle-aware manipulation as a controlled probe of geometry-dependent physical feasibility. Empirical results show that augmenting VLA training with feasibility supervision improves both physical reliability and overall task performance, while also enhancing learning efficiency in the low-data regime. These findings indicate that explicit feasibility signals can effectively complement imitation-based VLA learning, highlighting their potential for developing more reliable VLA policies.

</details>


### [103] [Learning to Correct: Calibrated Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Attempt Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17912)
*Muhammed Emrullah Ildiz,Halil Alperen Gozeten,Ege Onur Taga,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出CAL-GRPO方法，通过校准的尝试级加权策略优化Verification@K奖励，解决传统加权方法梯度偏差问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理模型使用长思维链解决复杂问题，但需要多次尝试并依赖验证反馈。传统方法在优化Verification@K奖励时，简单按尝试成功/失败加权会导致梯度偏差，需要更有效的强化学习方法

Method: 提出Calibrated Attempt-Level (CAL) GRPO方法，设计加权策略获得无偏梯度同时保持低方差。该方法在每次尝试中利用验证反馈，通过校准的尝试级加权优化Verification@K奖励

Result: 理论分析揭示了每次尝试奖励如何影响训练和最终Verification@K性能。在合成和真实数据上的实验、基线和消融研究验证了CAL-GRPO相比vanilla GRPO和朴素加权方法的优势

Conclusion: CAL-GRPO通过校准的尝试级加权策略有效解决了Verification@K奖励优化中的梯度偏差问题，在多次尝试推理任务中表现出更好的性能

Abstract: State-of-the-art reasoning models utilize long chain-of-thought (CoT) to solve increasingly complex problems using more test-time computation. In this work, we explore a long CoT setting where the model makes up to K successive attempts at solving a problem, in which each attempt is allowed to build on earlier ones after the model receives a hard verifier feedback. This motivates RL methods that can harness per-attempt rewards by carefully weighting individual attempts. We study optimizing the Verification@K reward (the model succeeds by the K-th attempt) and show that naively weighing the attempts by their pass/fail results in biased gradients. We introduce Calibrated Attempt-Level (CAL) GRPO by devising a weighing strategy to obtain unbiased gradients while maintaining small variance. Our theory reveals how incorporating per-attempt rewards influence the training and the eventual Verification@K performance. Experiments, baselines, and ablations on synthetic and real data corroborate our theory and the benefits of CAL-GRPO over vanilla GRPO as well as naive weighting.

</details>


### [104] [Fisher Decorator: Refining Flow Policy via A Local Transport Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17919)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Haoyu Wang,Wenxuan Yuan,Ziyan Wang,Zonghao Chen,Li Zeng,Zhuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于几何视角的离线强化学习新框架，通过引入各向异性优化解决现有流策略中L2正则化与Wasserstein距离不匹配的问题，实现了更好的策略表达性、最优性和效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流匹配的离线强化学习方法面临表达性、最优性和效率之间的权衡问题。特别是，这些方法将L2正则化解释为2-Wasserstein距离的上界，这在离线设置中存在根本性几何不匹配：行为策略流形本质上是各向异性的，而L2正则化（或W2上界）是各向同性且对密度不敏感的，导致优化方向系统性错位。

Method: 从几何视角重新审视离线强化学习，将策略精炼表述为局部传输映射：初始流策略加上残差位移。通过分析诱导的密度变换，推导出由Fisher信息矩阵控制的KL约束目标的局部二次近似，得到可处理的各向异性优化公式。利用流速度中嵌入的得分函数，获得相应的二次约束以实现高效优化。

Result: 理论分析表明，先前方法的最优性差距源于其各向同性近似。相比之下，本文框架在最优解的可证明邻域内实现了可控的近似误差。在多个离线强化学习基准测试中取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入几何视角和各向异性优化，本文提出的Fisher-Decorator框架有效解决了现有流策略中的正则化不匹配问题，在表达性、最优性和效率之间取得了更好的平衡，为离线强化学习提供了新的理论见解和实用方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in flow-based offline reinforcement learning (RL) have achieved strong performance by parameterizing policies via flow matching. However, they still face critical trade-offs among expressiveness, optimality, and efficiency. In particular, existing flow policies interpret the $L_2$ regularization as an upper bound of the 2-Wasserstein distance ($W_2$), which can be problematic in offline settings. This issue stems from a fundamental geometric mismatch: the behavioral policy manifold is inherently anisotropic, whereas the $L_2$ (or upper bound of $W_2$) regularization is isotropic and density-insensitive, leading to systematically misaligned optimization directions. To address this, we revisit offline RL from a geometric perspective and show that policy refinement can be formulated as a local transport map: an initial flow policy augmented by a residual displacement. By analyzing the induced density transformation, we derive a local quadratic approximation of the KL-constrained objective governed by the Fisher information matrix, enabling a tractable anisotropic optimization formulation. By leveraging the score function embedded in the flow velocity, we obtain a corresponding quadratic constraint for efficient optimization. Our results reveal that the optimality gap in prior methods arises from their isotropic approximation. In contrast, our framework achieves a controllable approximation error within a provable neighborhood of the optimal solution. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across diverse offline RL benchmarks. See project page: https://github.com/ARC0127/Fisher-Decorator.

</details>


### [105] [HEALing Entropy Collapse: Enhancing Exploration in Few-Shot RLVR via Hybrid-Domain Entropy Dynamics Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17928)
*Zhanyu Liu,Qingguo Hu,Ante Wang,Chenqing Liu,Zhishang Xiang,Hui Li,Delai Qiu,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEAL框架通过引入高价值通用领域数据和熵动态对齐机制，有效缓解低资源场景下强化学习验证奖励（RLVR）的熵崩溃问题，提升少样本推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法主要假设高资源设置，但在低资源场景下，RLVR容易出现更严重的熵崩溃问题，这会限制探索并降低推理性能。需要专门针对少样本RLVR的解决方案。

Method: 提出混合领域熵动态对齐（HEAL）框架：1）选择性引入高价值通用领域数据以促进多样化探索；2）提出熵动态对齐（EDA）奖励机制，在目标领域和通用领域之间对齐轨迹级熵动态，捕捉熵大小和细粒度变化。

Result: 在多个领域的实验中，HEAL一致提升了少样本RLVR性能。仅使用32个目标领域样本时，HEAL匹配甚至超越了使用1K目标领域样本的全样本RLVR。

Conclusion: HEAL通过结合通用领域数据和熵动态对齐，有效缓解了少样本RLVR中的熵崩溃问题，显著提升了低资源场景下的推理性能，为资源受限环境中的强化学习训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) has proven effective for training reasoning-oriented large language models, but existing methods largely assume high-resource settings with abundant training data. In low-resource scenarios, RLVR is prone to more severe entropy collapse, which substantially limits exploration and degrades reasoning performance. To address this issue, we propose Hybrid-domain Entropy dynamics ALignment (HEAL), a framework tailored for few-shot RLVR. HEAL first selectively incorporates high-value general-domain data to promote more diverse exploration. Then, we introduce Entropy Dynamics Alignment (EDA), a reward mechanism that aligns trajectory-level entropy dynamics between the target and general domains, capturing both entropy magnitude and fine-grained variation. Through this alignment, EDA not only further mitigates entropy collapse but also encourages the policy to acquire more diverse exploration behaviors from the general domain. Experiments across multiple domains show that HEAL consistently improves few-shot RLVR performance. Notably, using only 32 target-domain samples, HEAL matches or even surpasses full-shot RLVR trained with 1K target-domain samples.

</details>


### [106] [How Much Cache Does Reasoning Need? Depth-Cache Tradeoffs in KV-Compressed Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17935)
*Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Transformer推理中KV缓存的压缩极限，通过k-hop指针追踪分析了缓存大小、注意力维度等参数对推理深度的理论影响，揭示了带宽障碍和自适应缓存策略的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer推理过程中KV缓存是主要的内存瓶颈，但理论上不清楚在多大程度上可以压缩KV缓存而不影响多步推理能力。现有研究缺乏对KV缓存压缩极限的理论理解。

Method: 采用k-hop指针追踪任务，在n个token上分析共享KV缓存大小为s、注意力维度为m、H个头、p-bit精度的Transformer。使用满足局部性保持的缓存控制器（所有标准KV压缩方法都满足）。通过理论分析建立深度下界和上界。

Result: 1. 提出乘积深度下界猜想：L = Ω(⌈k/s⌉·⌈log₂n/(Hmp)⌉)，并证明了匹配的上界L = O(min(k, ⌈k/s⌉log s)·log n/(mp))和最大下界L = Ω(max(⌈k/s⌉, log n/(Hmp)))。
2. 发现带宽障碍：当Hmp ≲ log n时乘积界限才有效，一旦Hmp ≥ log₂n，基于每窗口可区分性计数的下界无法超过⌈k/s⌉。
3. 自适应与随机缓存错误率对比：自适应局部性保持缓存实现Pr[E] = s/n（与T无关），而随机缓存错误率呈指数增长，解释了为什么多跳推理中基于频率的驱逐策略优于随机驱逐。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩存在理论极限，乘积深度下界揭示了缓存大小与注意力维度之间的权衡关系。自适应缓存策略在保持多步推理能力方面显著优于随机策略，这为实际KV缓存压缩算法的设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the dominant memory bottleneck during Transformer inference, yet little is known theoretically about how aggressively it can be compressed before multi-step reasoning degrades. We study this through $k$-hop pointer chasing on $n$ tokens under a shared KV cache of size $s$, attention dimension $m$, $H$ heads, $p$-bit precision, and a locality-respecting cache controller (satisfied by all standard KV-compression methods). We give three results.
  (1) Product depth lower bound (conjectured). We conjecture that any such Transformer ($n \geq 4k$, $s \leq \sqrt{n}/4$) requires depth $L = Ω(\lceil k/s \rceil \cdot \lceil \log_2 n/(Hmp) \rceil)$, and isolate the sole remaining gap as a probabilistic step on the joint distribution of cache trace and pointer chain. Unconditionally, we prove a matching upper bound $L = O(\min(k, \lceil k/s \rceil \log s) \cdot \log n/(mp))$ via windowed pointer doubling, and a max-bound $L = Ω(\max(\lceil k/s \rceil, \log n/(Hmp)))$. Closing the conjecture amounts to upgrading max to product.
  (2) Bandwidth barrier. The product bound binds only when $Hmp \lesssim \log n$. Any lower bound provable via per-window distinguishability counting -- including reachability, bandwidth, and combinations -- cannot exceed $\lceil k/s \rceil$ once $Hmp \geq \log_2 n$. Breaking this requires lifting unconditional communication-complexity bounds for pointer chasing to Cache-Transformer depth.
  (3) Adaptive vs oblivious error scaling. Under random cache over $T = \lceil \log_2 k \rceil$ doubling stages, oblivious caches give $\Pr[\mathcal{E}] \leq (s/(n-T))^T + 2T^3/n$ (exponential in $T$), while adaptive locality-respecting caches achieve $\Pr[\mathcal{E}] = s/n$ exactly, independent of $T$. The $Ω((n/s)^{T-1})$ separation explains why heavy-hitter eviction empirically dominates random eviction for multi-hop reasoning.

</details>


### [107] [Federated Rule Ensemble Method in Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17956)
*Ke Wan,Kensuke Tanioka,Toshio Shimokawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦RuleFit框架，在保护隐私的分布式环境中构建统一且可解释的全局模型，通过差分隐私直方图预处理、本地规则生成和联邦双平均算法实现稀疏变量选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗机器学习需要大规模多样化数据集，但单机构数据有限，跨机构整合受隐私法规和数据所有权限制。现有联邦学习方法架构复杂且缺乏可解释性，限制了临床适用性。

Method: 1) 基于差分隐私直方图的预处理，估计共享截断值以实现一致的规则定义；2) 使用共享截断值的梯度提升决策树进行本地规则生成；3) 通过ℓ1正则化优化和联邦双平均算法进行系数估计，实现稀疏和一致的变量选择。

Result: 模拟研究中，该方法性能与集中式RuleFit相当，优于现有联邦学习方法。实际数据分析显示其能提供可解释的见解和具有竞争力的预测准确性。

Conclusion: 提出的联邦RuleFit框架为联邦学习环境提供了实用有效的可解释和可靠建模解决方案，平衡了隐私保护与模型性能及可解释性需求。

Abstract: Machine learning has become integral to medical research and is increasingly applied in clinical settings to support diagnosis and decision-making; however, its effectiveness depends on access to large, diverse datasets, which are limited within single institutions. Although integrating data across institutions can address this limitation, privacy regulations and data ownership constraints hinder these efforts. Federated learning enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data; however, most methods rely on complex architectures that lack interpretability, limiting clinical applicability. Therefore, we proposed a federated RuleFit framework to construct a unified and interpretable global model for distributed environments. It integrates three components: preprocessing based on differentially private histograms to estimate shared cutoff values, enabling consistent rule definitions and reducing heterogeneity across clients; local rule generation using gradient boosting decision trees with shared cutoffs; and coefficient estimation via $\ell_1$-regularized optimization using a Federated Dual Averaging algorithm for sparse and consistent variable selection. In simulation studies, the proposed method achieved a performance comparable to that of centralized RuleFit while outperforming existing federated approaches. Real-world analysis demonstrated its ability to provide interpretable insights with competitive predictive accuracy. Therefore, the proposed framework offers a practical and effective solution for interpretable and reliable modeling in federated learning environments.

</details>


### [108] [Online Conformal Prediction with Adversarial Semi-bandit Feedback via Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17984)
*Junyoung Yang,Kyungmin Kim,Sangdon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于在线共形预测的对抗性赌博机方法，处理部分反馈场景，其中真实标签仅在预测集内时被揭示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线共形预测方法通常假设完全反馈设置（总能观察到真实标签），但在实际安全关键系统中，真实标签可能仅在预测集内时才被揭示，这构成了部分反馈的更具挑战性场景

Method: 将在线共形预测建模为对抗性赌博机问题，将每个候选预测集视为一个臂，基于现有对抗性赌博机算法开发新方法，通过建立与学习者遗憾的明确连接来保证长期覆盖率

Result: 在独立同分布和非独立同分布设置下实证验证了方法的有效性，成功控制了误覆盖率，同时保持了合理的预测集大小

Conclusion: 提出的对抗性赌博机方法能够处理在线共形预测中的部分反馈问题，在更具挑战性的自适应对手设置下实现了长期覆盖率保证，为安全关键系统中的不确定性量化提供了新工具

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is crucial in safety-critical systems, where decisions must be made under uncertainty. In particular, we consider the problem of online uncertainty quantification, where data points arrive sequentially. Online conformal prediction is a principled online uncertainty quantification method that dynamically constructs a prediction set at each time step. While existing methods for online conformal prediction provide long-run coverage guarantees without any distributional assumptions, they typically assume a full feedback setting in which the true label is always observed. In this paper, we propose a novel learning method for online conformal prediction with partial feedback from an adaptive adversary-a more challenging setup where the true label is revealed only when it lies inside the constructed prediction set. Specifically, we formulate online conformal prediction as an adversarial bandit problem by treating each candidate prediction set as an arm. Building on an existing algorithm for adversarial bandits, our method achieves a long-run coverage guarantee by explicitly establishing its connection to the regret of the learner. Finally, we empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in both independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) and non-i.i.d. settings, showing that it successfully controls the miscoverage rate while maintaining a reasonable size of the prediction set.

</details>


### [109] [Clusterability-Based Assessment of Potentially Noisy Views for Multi-View Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18024)
*Mudi Jiang,Jiahui Zhou,Xinying Liu,Zengyou He,Zhikui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个专门针对多视图数据的聚类性评分方法MVCS，用于聚类前评估视图质量并检测噪声视图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图聚类中不同视图质量差异大，低质量视图会损害聚类性能，现有方法主要在聚类过程中通过视图加权或噪声鲁棒优化处理，缺乏聚类前数据层面的评估

Method: 提出多视图聚类性评分MVCS，通过三个互补组件量化多视图数据中潜在聚类相关结构的强度：单视图结构聚类性、联合空间聚类性和跨视图邻域一致性

Result: 实验表明噪声视图会显著降低聚类性能，与单视图聚类性度量相比，MVCS能更有效地支持噪声视图分析和检测

Conclusion: MVCS是首个专门为多视图数据设计的聚类性评分方法，能在聚类前有效评估视图质量并检测噪声视图，提高多视图聚类性能

Abstract: In multi-view clustering, the quality of different views may vary substantially, and low-quality or degraded views can impair overall clustering performance. However, existing studies mainly address this issue within the clustering process through view weighting or noise-robust optimization, while paying limited attention to data-level assessment before clustering. In this paper, we study the problem of pre-clustering noisy-view analysis in multi-view data from a clusterability perspective. To this end, we propose a Multi-View Clusterability Score (MVCS), which quantifies the strength of latent cluster-related structures in multi-view data through three complementary components: per-view structural clusterability, joint-space clusterability, and cross-view neighborhood consistency. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first clusterability score specifically designed for multi-view data. We further use it to perform potentially noisy view analysis and noisy-view detection before clustering. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that noisy views can significantly degrade clustering performance, and that, compared with existing clusterability measures designed for single-view data, the proposed method more effectively supports noisy-view analysis and detection.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Real-Time ECG and EMG Modeling on $μ$ NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18067)
*Josh Millar,Ashok Samraj Thangarajan,Soumyajit Chatterjee,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhysioLite：一种轻量级、NPU兼容的ECG/EMG信号分析模型架构和训练框架，在保持与最先进Transformer模型相当性能的同时，模型大小减少90%以上，适合资源受限的微控制器硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然神经处理单元（NPUs）和其他低功耗加速器的小型化使其能够集成到微控制器级可穿戴硬件中，但生理信号分析在此类硬件上仍然不可行。现有的Transformer模型虽然性能优异，但模型过大且动态注意力操作与μNPUs不兼容。

Method: 提出PhysioLite架构和训练框架，采用可学习小波滤波器组、CPU卸载的位置编码和硬件感知的层设计，使模型兼容NPU硬件。模型经过8位量化后仅约370KB。

Result: PhysioLite在ECG和EMG基准测试中达到与最先进Transformer基础模型相当的性能，同时模型大小仅为后者的<10%。在MAX78000和HX6538 WE2 μNPUs上验证了其延迟和资源消耗，证明其在受限电池供电硬件上的可行性。

Conclusion: PhysioLite实现了在资源受限硬件上进行生理信号分析的突破，为可穿戴设备上的实时、离线、隐私保护的推理提供了可行解决方案，推动了边缘计算在医疗健康领域的应用。

Abstract: The miniaturisation of neural processing units (NPUs) and other low-power accelerators has enabled their integration into microcontroller-scale wearable hardware, supporting near-real-time, offline, and privacy-preserving inference. Yet physiological signal analysis has remained infeasible on such hardware; recent Transformer-based models show state-of-the-art performance but are prohibitively large for resource- and power-constrained hardware and incompatible with $μ$ NPUs due to their dynamic attention operations. We introduce PhysioLite, a lightweight, NPU-compatible model architecture and training framework for ECG/EMG signal analysis. Using learnable wavelet filter banks, CPU-offloaded positional encoding, and hardware-aware layer design, PhysioLite reaches performance comparable to state-of-the-art Transformer-based foundation models on ECG and EMG benchmarks, while being <10% of the size ($\sim$370KB with 8-bit quantization). We also profile its component-wise latency and resource consumption on both the MAX78000 and HX6538 WE2 $μ$ NPUs, demonstrating its viability for signal analysis on constrained, battery-powered hardware. We release our model(s) and training framework at: https://github.com/j0shmillar/physiolite.

</details>


### [111] [Implicit neural representations as a coordinate-based framework for continuous environmental field reconstruction from sparse ecological observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18083)
*Agnieszka Pregowska,Hazem M. Kalaji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了隐式神经表示（INRs）作为坐标建模框架，用于从稀疏和不规则的环境观测数据中学习连续空间和时空场，在物种分布重建、物候动态和形态分割三个场景中验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境建模和生物多样性信息学面临的核心挑战是从稀疏和不规则观测中重建连续环境场。许多生态数据集在空间和时间上具有异质性，使得基于网格的方法难以跨领域扩展或泛化。

Method: 使用隐式神经表示（INRs）作为坐标建模框架，直接从坐标输入学习连续空间和时空场。在三个代表性建模场景中进行分析：物种分布重建、物候动态和从开放生物多样性数据衍生的形态分割。

Result: 神经场提供了稳定的连续表示，具有可预测的计算成本，补充了经典平滑器和基于树的方法。它们在插值行为、空间一致性和计算特性方面表现出色，包括可扩展性、分辨率无关查询和架构归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 坐标神经场可作为灵活的表示层，集成到环境建模流程和探索性分析框架中，适用于大规模、不规则采样的数据集。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous environmental fields from sparse and irregular observations remains a central challenge in environmental modelling and biodiversity informatics. Many ecological datasets are heterogeneous in space and time, making grid-based approaches difficult to scale or generalise across domains. Here, we evaluate implicit neural representations (INRs) as a coordinate-based modelling framework for learning continuous spatial and spatio-temporal fields directly from coordinate inputs. We analyse their behaviour across three representative modelling scenarios: species distribution reconstruction, phenological dynamics, and morphological segmentation derived from open biodiversity data. Beyond predictive performance, we examine interpolation behaviour, spatial coherence, and computational characteristics relevant for environmental modelling workflows, including scalability, resolution-independent querying, and architectural inductive bias. Results show that neural fields provide stable continuous representations with predictable computational cost, complementing classical smoothers and tree-based approaches. These findings position coordinate-based neural fields as a flexible representation layer that can be integrated into environmental modelling pipelines and exploratory analysis frameworks for large, irregularly sampled datasets.

</details>


### [112] [Predicting LLM Compression Degradation from Spectral Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18085)
*Mingxue,Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现压缩比与稳定秩的乘积（γ·ρ_s）能有效预测低秩压缩对LLM性能的影响，支持"先预测后压缩"的工作流程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型的低秩压缩计算成本高昂，需要一种能在实际压缩前预测性能退化的方法，以避免不必要的计算开销

Method: 系统分析了Qwen3和Gemma3模型家族，使用四种代表性低秩压缩方法（vanilla SVD、两种ASVD变体、SVD-LLM），研究稳定秩和信息密度等指标对性能退化的影响

Result: 压缩比与稳定秩的乘积（γ·ρ_s）是性能退化的稳健预测因子，在注意力层和MLP层分别达到0.890和0.839的留一交叉验证皮尔逊相关系数

Conclusion: 提出了"先预测后压缩"的工作流程：从权重计算γ·ρ_s，估计性能退化，只对有利配置进行压缩计算，为高效模型压缩提供了理论基础和实践指导

Abstract: Matrix-level low-rank compression is a promising way to reduce the cost of large language models, but running compression and evaluating the resulting models on language tasks can be prohibitively expensive. Can compression-induced degradation be predicted before committing to this compute? We systematically analyze the Qwen3 and Gemma3 model families across four representative low-rank compression methods: vanilla SVD, two ASVD variants, and SVD-LLM. We find that stable rank and information density, measured in bits per parameter, dominate performance degradation.
