The interaction term $γ\cdot \barρ_s$, defined as compression ratio times stable rank, is a robust predictor of accuracy degradation, achieving leave-one-out cross-validation Pearson correlations of $0.890$ for attention layers and $0.839$ for MLP layers. We provide theoretical intuition for why this predictor succeeds by connecting it to standard SVD truncation bounds and error composition mechanisms in transformer layers. These findings enable a predict-then-compress workflow: compute $γ\cdot \barρ_s$ from weights, estimate degradation, and invest compute only in desirable configurations.

</details>


### [113] [Towards E-Value Based Stopping Rules for Bayesian Deep Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18089)
*Emanuel Sommer,Rickmer Schulte,Sarah Deubner,Julius Kobialka,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于E值的停止规则，用于确定贝叶斯深度集成中MCMC采样的最佳时长，以在显著改善初始优化集成基线的同时避免过度计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯深度集成结合了深度集成的鲁棒性和多链MCMC的灵活性，但MCMC采样成本高昂。现有研究表明在优化后的深度集成基础上进行采样能带来显著改善，但缺乏确定采样时长的实用方法。

Method: 提出基于E值的停止规则，将集成构建表述为序列任意时间有效假设检验，通过检验MCMC是否比强基线有显著改善来决定是否停止采样。

Result: 实证研究表明该方法有效，通常只需要完整链预算的一小部分就能获得显著改善，为贝叶斯深度集成提供了实用的采样停止准则。

Conclusion: 基于E值的停止规则为贝叶斯深度集成中的MCMC采样提供了理论严谨且实用的停止准则，能在保证性能改善的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Bayesian Deep Ensembles (BDEs) represent a powerful approach for uncertainty quantification in deep learning, combining the robustness of Deep Ensembles (DEs) with flexible multi-chain MCMC. While DEs are affordable in most deep learning settings, (long) sampling of Bayesian neural networks can be prohibitively costly. Yet, adding sampling after optimizing the DEs has been shown to yield significant improvements. This leaves a critical practical question: How long should the sequential sampling process continue to yield significant improvements over the initial optimized DE baseline? To tackle this question, we propose a stopping rule based on E-values. We formulate the ensemble construction as a sequential anytime-valid hypothesis test, providing a principled way to decide whether or not to reject the null hypothesis that MCMC offers no improvement over a strong baseline, to early stop the sampling. Empirically, we study this approach for diverse settings. Our results demonstrate the efficacy of our approach and reveal that only a fraction of the full-chain budget is often required.

</details>


### [114] [Generalization Boundaries of Fine-Tuned Small Language Models for Graph Structural Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18092)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了用于图属性估计的微调小语言模型在超出训练条件时的泛化能力，重点关注图尺寸和图族分布两个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然微调的小语言模型在图属性估计任务上表现出良好的分布内性能，但其在训练条件之外的泛化能力尚未得到充分理解。研究旨在系统探索这些模型在结构推理任务中的泛化边界。

Method: 采用受控实验设置，使用3个3-4B参数规模的指令微调模型和两种图序列化格式。评估模型在显著大于训练范围的图尺寸上的性能，以及在不同随机图族之间的泛化能力。

Result: 微调模型在不同结构图族之间保持了强序一致性，即使在训练中未见的大尺寸输入上也能正确按结构属性对图进行排序。不同架构模型表现出特定的性能退化模式。

Conclusion: 研究明确了微调小语言模型在哪些情况下能够可靠泛化，为它们在基于图的推理任务中的应用提供了实证基础，揭示了模型在结构推理任务中的泛化边界和局限性。

Abstract: Small language models fine-tuned for graph property estimation have demonstrated strong in-distribution performance, yet their generalization capabilities beyond training conditions remain poorly understood. In this work, we systematically investigate the boundaries of structural inference in fine-tuned small language models along two generalization axes - graph size and graph family distribution - and assess domain-learning capability on real-world graph benchmarks. Using a controlled experimental setup with three instruction-tuned models in the 3-4B parameter class and two graph serialization formats, we evaluate performance on graphs substantially larger than the training range and across held-out random graph families. Our results show that fine-tuned models maintain strong ordinal consistency across structurally distinct graph families and continue to rank graphs by structural properties on inputs substantially larger than those seen during training, with distinct architecture-specific degradation profiles. These findings delineate where fine-tuned small language models generalize reliably, providing empirical grounding for their use in graph-based reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [115] [LoRaQ: Optimized Low Rank Approximation for 4-bit Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18117)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Sophie Yáng Shen,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRaQ是一种数据无关的低秩近似量化方法，首次实现了完全低于16位的扩散变换器量化管道，在相同内存开销下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法在4位量化时生成性能显著下降，而低秩近似方法需要高精度分支（W16A16）和大量数据依赖校准，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出LoRaQ（低秩近似量化），通过优化量化误差补偿实现数据无关校准，允许低秩分支本身也被量化，支持W8A8、W6A6、W4A8等多种混合精度配置。

Result: 在Pixart-Σ和SANA模型上，LoRaQ在相同内存开销下优于现有方法，首次实现了完全低于16位的量化管道，混合精度配置（如W4主层+W8A8低秩分支）表现优异。

Conclusion: LoRaQ突破了低秩近似量化需要高精度分支的限制，实现了数据无关校准和完全量化架构，与现代混合精度硬件兼容，为资源受限硬件部署扩散变换器提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large diffusion transformers on resource-constrained hardware, but aggressive 4-bit quantization significantly degrades generative performance. Low-rank approximation methods have emerged as a promising solution by appending auxiliary linear branches to restore performance. However, current state-of-the-art approaches assume these branches must retain high precision (W16A16) and rely on heavy, data-dependent calibration for initialization. We challenge both limitations with LoRaQ (Low-Rank Approximated Quantization), a simple, data-free calibration approach that optimizes quantization error compensation. By overcoming the need for high-precision branches, LoRaQ enables the first fully sub-16 bit pipeline, allowing the low-rank branch itself to be quantized. We demonstrate that, at equal memory overhead, LoRaQ outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in their native implementations on Pixart-$Σ$ and SANA. We also analyze mixed-precision configurations, showing that setups such as W8A8, W6A6, and W4A8 for the low-rank branch, alongside a W4 main layer, yield superior results while maintaining a fully quantized architecture compatible with modern mixed-precision hardware.

</details>


### [116] [An `Inverse' Experimental Framework to Estimate Market Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18130)
*Thomas Asikis,Heinrich Nax*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于订单簿数据预测市场效率（特别是配置效率）的框架，通过量化归一化技术处理非结构化市场数据，利用线性回归和梯度提升树等模型进行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字市场作为关键基础设施缺乏系统性的性能测量框架，传统订单簿数据无法反映真实的市场效率，因为买卖报价不代表真实的交易意愿，需要新的方法从复杂市场数据中评估市场效率和公平性。

Method: 采用实验数据"反转"传统的诱导价值方法，提出基于分位数的归一化技术处理非结构化、非平稳、非线性的订单簿数据，构建包括线性回归和梯度提升树在内的预测模型，利用供需模型的量化输入预测配置效率。

Result: 模型能够仅从最早的买卖报价中合理准确地预测配置效率，随着实际价格数据的增加预测效果改善，不同目标和市场类型的预测技术表现存在差异。

Conclusion: 该框架具有应用于真实市场数据的潜力，能够在任何交易实现之前提供对市场效率和性能的宝贵洞察，为算法治理决策提供信息支持。

Abstract: Digital marketplaces processing billions of dollars annually represent critical infrastructure in sociotechnical ecosystems, yet their performance optimization lacks principled measurement frameworks that can inform algorithmic governance decisions regarding market efficiency and fairness from complex market data. By looking at orderbook data from double auction markets alone, because bids and asks do not represent true maximum willingnesses to buy and true minimum willingnesses to sell, there is little an economist can say about the market's actual performance in terms of allocative efficiency. We turn to experimental data to address this issue, `inverting' the standard induced value approach of double auction experiments. Our aim is to predict key market features relevant to market efficiency, particularly allocative efficiency, using orderbook data only -- specifically bids, asks and price realizations, but not the induced reservation values -- as early as possible. Since there is no established model of strategically optimal behavior in these markets, and because orderbook data is highly unstructured, non-stationary and non-linear, we propose quantile-based normalization techniques that help us build general predictive models. We develop and train several models, including linear regressions and gradient boosting trees, leveraging quantile-based input from the underlying supply-demand model. Our models can predict allocative efficiency with reasonable accuracy from the earliest bids and asks, and these predictions improve with additional realized price data. The performance of the prediction techniques varies by target and market type. Our framework holds significant potential for application to real-world market data, offering valuable insights into market efficiency and performance, even prior to any trade realizations.

</details>


### [117] [Does "Do Differentiable Simulators Give Better Policy Gradients?'' Give Better Policy Gradients?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18161)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Manato Yaguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了策略梯度强化学习中不连续动态导致的梯度估计偏差问题，提出了两种解决方案：DDCG用于检测不连续区域并切换估计器，IVW-H用于方差控制，发现实际应用中方差控制比不连续检测更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略梯度强化学习中，可微分模型提供的一阶梯度估计能加速学习，但不连续动态会导致偏差并削弱一阶估计器的效果。先前工作通过REINFORCE零阶估计器构建置信区间来检测不连续性，但该方法噪声大、需要任务特定超参数调优且样本效率低。本文旨在探究这种偏差是否是主要障碍，以及需要哪些最小化修复。

Method: 1. DDCG（Discontinuity Detection and Gradient Switching）：轻量级测试方法，在不平滑区域切换估计器，仅需单个超参数，在小样本下仍保持可靠。2. IVW-H（Inverse-Variance Weighting per-step）：每步逆方差加权实现，无需显式不连续检测即可稳定方差，在可微分机器人控制任务中表现良好。

Result: DDCG在标准不连续设置中实现了稳健性能，IVW-H在可微分机器人控制任务中产生了强劲结果。研究发现：虽然估计器切换在受控研究中提高了鲁棒性，但在实际部署中，仔细的方差控制往往更为关键。

Conclusion: 不连续动态导致的偏差问题可以通过轻量级估计器切换（DDCG）有效处理，但在实际应用中，方差控制（如IVW-H）通常比不连续检测更为重要。这表明解决策略梯度学习中的不连续性问题时，应优先考虑方差稳定化方法。

Abstract: In policy gradient reinforcement learning, access to a differentiable model enables 1st-order gradient estimation that accelerates learning compared to relying solely on derivative-free 0th-order estimators. However, discontinuous dynamics cause bias and undermine the effectiveness of 1st-order estimators. Prior work addressed this bias by constructing a confidence interval around the REINFORCE 0th-order gradient estimator and using these bounds to detect discontinuities. However, the REINFORCE estimator is notoriously noisy, and we find that this method requires task-specific hyperparameter tuning and has low sample efficiency. This paper asks whether such bias is the primary obstacle and what minimal fixes suffice. First, we re-examine standard discontinuous settings from prior work and introduce DDCG, a lightweight test that switches estimators in nonsmooth regions; with a single hyperparameter, DDCG achieves robust performance and remains reliable with small samples. Second, on differentiable robotics control tasks, we present IVW-H, a per-step inverse-variance implementation that stabilizes variance without explicit discontinuity detection and yields strong results. Together, these findings indicate that while estimator switching improves robustness in controlled studies, careful variance control often dominates in practical deployments.

</details>


### [118] [FSEVAL: Feature Selection Evaluation Toolbox and Dashboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18227)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FSEVAL：一个特征选择评估工具箱和可视化仪表板，旨在标准化、统一化特征选择算法的评估流程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征选择是机器学习和数据挖掘的基本任务，但缺乏标准化的评估工具。现有评估方法分散，难以全面比较不同特征选择算法的性能，研究者需要统一的评估框架来简化评估流程。

Method: 开发FSEVAL工具箱，包含特征选择评估框架和可视化仪表板。该工具支持监督和无监督设置，集成多种评估指标，提供标准化接口，使研究者能够轻松进行全面的算法评估。

Result: FSEVAL提供了一个统一的评估平台，简化了特征选择算法的比较过程。通过可视化仪表板，用户可以直观地分析算法性能，支持多种评估指标的综合对比。

Conclusion: FSEVAL填补了特征选择领域标准化评估工具的空白，为研究者提供了便捷、全面的评估解决方案，有望促进特征选择算法的研究和应用发展。

Abstract: Feature selection is a fundamental machine learning and data mining task, involved with discriminating redundant features from informative ones. It is an attempt to address the curse of dimensionality by removing the redundant features, while unlike dimensionality reduction methods, preserving explainability. Feature selection is conducted in both supervised and unsupervised settings, with different evaluation metrics employed to determine which feature selection algorithm is the best. In this paper, we propose FSEVAL, a feature selection evaluation toolbox accompanied with a visualization dashboard, with the goal to make it easy to comprehensively evaluate feature selection algorithms. FSEVAL aims to provide a standardized, unified, evaluation and visualization toolbox to help the researchers working in the field, conduct extensive and comprehensive evaluation of feature selection algorithms with ease.

</details>


### [119] [Towards Disentangled Preference Optimization Dynamics Beyond Likelihood Displacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18239)
*Wei Chen,Yubing Wu,Junmei Yang,Delu Zeng,Qibin Zhao,John Paisley,Min Chen,Zhou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了奖励校准(RC)方法，通过激励分数分解识别解纠缠带(DB)条件，自适应调整选择与拒绝更新的平衡，缓解偏好优化中的似然位移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于边际的偏好优化目标在抑制被拒绝响应的同时也会抑制被选择的响应（似然位移现象），缺乏通用的防止机制。需要一种能够保持获胜响应质量的同时抑制失败响应的训练方法。

Method: 1) 提出统一的激励分数分解框架，揭示不同目标共享相同局部更新方向；2) 分析选择/拒绝似然动态，识别解纠缠带(DB)条件；3) 基于DB设计即插即用的奖励校准(RC)方法，自适应重新平衡选择与拒绝更新。

Result: 实验结果表明，RC能够引导训练朝向更解纠缠的动态，并在多种目标函数下改善下游任务性能。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过激励分数分解和DB条件分析，提出的RC方法能够有效缓解偏好优化中的似然位移问题，实现"抑制失败者同时保持胜利者"的理想训练路径，提升模型对齐效果。

Abstract: Preference optimization is widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, many margin-based objectives suppress the chosen response along with the rejected one, a phenomenon known as likelihood displacement, and no general mechanism currently prevents this across objectives.
  We bridge this gap by presenting a unified \emph{incentive-score decomposition} of preference optimization, revealing that diverse objectives share identical local update directions and differ only in their scalar weighting coefficients.
  Building on this decomposition, by analyzing the dynamics of the chosen/rejected likelihoods, we identify the \emph{disentanglement band} (DB), a simple, testable condition that characterizes when training can avoid likelihood displacement by realizing the preferred pathway: suppressing the loser while maintaining the winner, possibly after an initial transient.
  Leveraging the DB, we propose a plug-and-play \emph{reward calibration} (RC) that adaptively rebalances chosen versus rejected updates to satisfy the DB and mitigate likelihood displacement, without redesigning the base objective.
  Empirical results show that RC steers training toward more disentangled dynamics and often improves downstream performance across a range of objectives. Our code is available at https://github.com/IceyWuu/DisentangledPreferenceOptimization.

</details>


### [120] [CAARL: In-Context Learning for Interpretable Co-Evolving Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18305)
*Etienne Tajeuna,Patrick Asante Owusu,Armelle Brun,Shengrui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAARL是一种基于大语言模型的协同演化时间序列预测方法，通过分解时间序列、构建依赖图和序列化叙事，提供可解释的预测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同演化时间序列预测方法难以处理复杂的依赖关系和非平稳动态，且缺乏可解释性。需要一种既能保持预测准确性又能提供透明推理过程的解决方案。

Method: 提出Context-Aware AR-LLM (CAARL)方法：1) 将时间序列分解为自回归段；2) 构建时间依赖图；3) 将依赖图序列化为叙事供LLM处理；4) 通过类似思维链的推理路径进行预测。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验验证了CAARL的有效性，表明它既能保持预测准确性，又能提供可解释的推理过程，成为最先进预测方法的竞争性替代方案。

Conclusion: CAARL为协同演化时间序列预测提供了一个可解释的框架，通过将预测与明确的推理轨迹联系起来，在保持准确性的同时增强了可解释性。

Abstract: In this paper we investigate forecasting coevolving time series that feature intricate dependencies and nonstationary dynamics by using an LLM Large Language Models approach We propose a novel modeling approach named ContextAware ARLLM CAARL that provides an interpretable framework to decode the contextual dynamics influencing changes in coevolving series CAARL decomposes time series into autoregressive segments constructs a temporal dependency graph and serializes this graph into a narrative to allow processing by LLM This design yields a chainofthoughtlike reasoning path where intermediate steps capture contextual dynamics and guide forecasts in a transparent manner By linking prediction to explicit reasoning traces CAARL enhances interpretability while maintaining accuracy Experiments on realworld datasets validate its effectiveness positioning CAARL as a competitive and interpretable alternative to stateoftheart forecasting methods

</details>


### [121] [Scale-free adaptive planning for deterministic dynamics & discounted rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18312)
*Peter L. Bartlett,Victor Gabillon,Jennifer Healey,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Platypoos算法解决确定性动态和随机奖励环境中的规划问题，无需已知奖励边界，自适应奖励函数的未知尺度和平滑度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在确定性动态和随机奖励环境中进行规划的问题，其中最优值函数未知且奖励无界，现有方法需要已知奖励尺度或边界

Method: 提出Platypoos算法，一种简单的无尺度规划算法，能够自适应奖励函数的未知尺度和平滑度，无需预先知道折扣因子和奖励尺度

Result: 提供了样本复杂度分析，改进先前工作，结果同时适用于广泛的折扣因子和奖励尺度范围，并建立了匹配的下界证明分析在常数因子内最优

Conclusion: Platypoos算法在确定性动态和随机奖励环境中实现了自适应规划，样本复杂度分析达到理论最优，无需先验知识

Abstract: We address the problem of planning in an environment with deterministic dynamics and stochastic rewards with discounted returns. The optimal value function is not known, nor are the rewards bounded. We propose Platypoos, a simple scale-free planning algorithm that adapts to the unknown scale and smoothness of the reward function. We provide a sample complexity analysis for Platypoos that improves upon prior work and holds simultaneously over a broad range of discount factors and reward scales, without the algorithm knowing them. We also establish a matching lower bound showing our analysis is optimal up to constants.

</details>


### [122] [Parkinson's Disease Detection via Self-Supervised Dual-Channel Cross-Attention on Bilateral Wrist-Worn IMU Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18372)
*Meheru Zannat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于自监督交叉注意力编码器的PD分类方法，使用手腕佩戴IMU传感器数据，在PADS数据集上实现了高准确率的健康对照vs帕金森病和帕金森病vs鉴别诊断分类，并验证了实时部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病临床诊断耗时且主观，需要更客观、高效的监测方法。可穿戴IMU传感器为被动监测提供了可能，但需要有效的算法来处理传感器数据并实现准确的疾病分类。

Method: 提出自监督交叉注意力编码器处理双侧手腕IMU信号，使用对比infoNCE损失进行表示学习，在PADS数据集（469名受试者）上进行训练和评估，包括PD、HC和DD三组分类任务。

Result: 有监督分类：HC vs PD平均准确率93.12%，PD vs DD准确率87.04%。自监督学习仅用20%标签数据：HC vs PD准确率93.56%，PD vs DD准确率92.50%。模型在树莓派CPU上平均推理时间48.32ms/窗口。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效区分帕金森病与健康对照，但区分帕金森病与其他神经退行性疾病更具挑战性。自监督表示学习在标签数据有限时表现优异，模型实时部署可行，为临床应用的转移学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is a chronic neurodegenerative disease. It shows multiple motor symptoms such as tremor, bradykinesia, postural instability, freezing of gait (FoG). PD is currently diagnosed clinically through physical exam by health-care professionals, which can be time consuming and highly subjective. Wearable IMU sensors has become a promising gateway for passive monitoring of PD patients. We propose a self-supervised cross-attention encoder that processes bilateral wrist-worn IMU signals from a public dataset called PADS, consisting of three groups, PD (Parkinson Disease), HC (Healthy Control) and DD (Differential Diagnosis) of a total of 469 subjects. We have achieved a mean accuracy of 93.12% for HC vs. PD classification and 87.04% for PD vs. DD classification. The results emphasize the clinical challenge of distinguishing Parkinson's from other neurodegenerative diseases. Self-supervised representation learning using contrastive infoNCE loss gained an accuracy of 93.56% for HC vs. PD and 92.50% for PD vs. DD using only 20% of labelled data. This demonstrates the effectiveness of our method in transfer learning for clinical use with minimal labels. The real-time applicability was tested by deploying the optimized model with a mean inference time of 48.32 ms per window on a Raspberry Pi CPU.

</details>


### [123] [Forecasting Ionospheric Irregularities on GNSS Lines of Sight Using Dynamic Graphs with Ephemeris Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18379)
*Mert Can Turkmen,Eng Leong Tan,Yee Hui Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于动态图的电离层不规则性预测模型IonoDGNN，利用卫星轨迹可预测性进行星历条件化，在GNSS数据上优于传统网格方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动的电离层预测模型基于网格化产品，无法保留卫星传感的时变采样结构。需要一种能够处理动态观测几何的方法来更准确地预测电离层不规则性。

Method: 将电离层建模为电离层穿刺点(IPP)上的动态图，图结构随卫星位置变化而演化。利用卫星轨迹的可预测性，预先构建预测时域内的图拓扑，实现星历条件化(ephemeris conditioning)。使用IonoDGNN模型进行5分钟间隔、最长2小时的ROTI定义的不规则性二元概率分类预测。

Result: IonoDGNN在2023年1月至2025年4月新加坡共址接收器对的GNSS数据上，Brier Skill Score达到0.49，PR-AUC达到0.75，比持续性预测在BSS上提升35%，PR-AUC提升52%。星历条件化对预测期间新出现的卫星至关重要（ROC AUC：0.95 vs. 0.52）。在模拟覆盖丢失情况下，模型通过空间消息传递保持预测能力。

Conclusion: 基于动态图在演化视线上的预测是电离层不规则性预测中网格表示的一种可行替代方案。模型和评估代码将在发表后发布。

Abstract: Most data-driven ionospheric forecasting models operate on gridded products, which do not preserve the time-varying sampling structure of satellite-based sensing. We instead model the ionosphere as a dynamic graph over ionospheric pierce points (IPPs), with connectivity that evolves as satellite positions change. Because satellite trajectories are predictable, the graph topology over the forecast horizon can be constructed in advance. We exploit this property to condition forecasts on the future graph structure, which we term ephemeris conditioning. This enables prediction on lines of sight that appear only in the forecast horizon. We evaluate our framework on multi-GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite System) data from a co-located receiver pair in Singapore spanning January 2023 through April 2025. The task is to forecast Rate of TEC Index (ROTI)-defined irregularities at 5-minute cadence up to 2 hours ahead as binary probabilistic classification per node. The resulting model, IonoDGNN, achieves a Brier Skill Score (BSS) of 0.49 and a precision-recall area under the curve (PR-AUC) of 0.75, improving over persistence by 35\% in BSS and 52\% in PR-AUC, with larger gains at longer lead times. Ablations confirm that graph structure and ephemeris conditioning each contribute meaningfully, with conditioning proving essential for satellites that rise during the forecast horizon (receiver operating characteristic AUC: 0.95 vs.\ 0.52 without). Under simulated coverage dropout, the model retains predictive skill on affected nodes through spatial message passing from observed neighbors. These results suggest that dynamic graph forecasting on evolving lines of sight is a viable alternative to grid-based representations for ionospheric irregularity forecasting. The model and evaluation code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [124] [Balance-Guided Sparse Identification of Multiscale Nonlinear PDEs with Small-coefficient Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18414)
*Zhenhua Dang,Lei Zhang,Long Wang,Guowei He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BG-SINDy方法通过基于主导平衡原理的项级ℓ₂,₀正则化，解决多尺度系统中小系数项难以识别的问题，采用渐进剪枝策略保留动态显著项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法在识别多尺度系统时面临挑战，因为动态显著项可能具有小系数，导致传统稀疏回归方法难以准确发现控制方程。

Method: 提出BG-SINDy方法，基于主导平衡原理将ℓ₀约束稀疏回归重新表述为项级ℓ₂,₀正则化问题，采用渐进剪枝策略交替进行最小二乘回归和可忽略项消除，根据项对控制方程平衡的相对贡献而非绝对系数大小进行排序。

Result: 在Korteweg-de Vries方程（小色散系数）、修正Burgers方程（消失超粘性）、修正Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程（多小系数项）和二维反应-扩散系统上的数值实验验证了BG-SINDy能够有效发现小系数项。

Conclusion: BG-SINDy为发现包含小系数项的控制方程提供了高效方法，通过基于平衡贡献而非系数大小的项选择策略，解决了多尺度系统中动态显著小系数项的识别难题。

Abstract: Data-driven discovery of governing equations has advanced significantly in recent years; however, existing methods often struggle in multiscale systems where dynamically significant terms may have small coefficients. Therefore, we propose Balance-Guided SINDy (BG-SINDy) inspired by the principle of dominant balance, which reformulates $\ell_0$-constrained sparse regression as a term-level $\ell_{2,0}$-regularized problem and solves it using a progressive pruning strategy. Terms are ranked according to their relative contributions to the governing equation balance rather than their absolute coefficient magnitudes. Based on this criterion, BG-SINDy alternates between least-squares regression and elimination of negligible terms, thereby preserving dynamically significant terms even when their coefficients are small. Numerical experiments on the Korteweg--de Vries equation with a small dispersion coefficient, a modified Burgers equation with vanishing hyperviscosity, a modified Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equation with multiple small-coefficient terms, and a two-dimensional reaction--diffusion system demonstrate the validity of BG-SINDy in discovering small-coefficient terms. The proposed method thus provides an efficient approach for discovering governing equations that contain small-coefficient terms.

</details>


### [125] [Knowing When to Quit: A Principled Framework for Dynamic Abstention in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18419)
*Hen Davidov,Nachshon Cohen,Oren Kalinsky,Yaron Fairstein,Guy Kushilevitz,Ram Yazdi,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于强化学习的动态中止生成方法，通过价值函数判断何时中止不正确的推理过程，在计算效率和准确性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型使用思维链推理时经常产生冗长但错误的回答，浪费大量计算资源。现有的中止方法主要在生成前或生成后决定是否中止，而动态生成过程中止可以在每个token位置提前终止无望的推理轨迹，但缺乏理论指导。

Method: 将动态中止建模为强化学习框架中的显式动作，通过中止奖励参数控制计算与信息之间的权衡。当价值函数低于该奖励时中止生成，并推导出近似价值函数的高效方法。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法在一般条件下严格优于自然基线。在数学推理和毒性避免任务上的实证结果支持理论，并展示了比现有方法更好的选择性准确性。

Conclusion: 提出了一个理论框架来指导动态中止决策，通过强化学习建模和高效的价值函数近似，实现了在计算效率和准确性之间的优化平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) using chain-of-thought reasoning often waste substantial compute by producing long, incorrect responses. Abstention can mitigate this by withholding outputs unlikely to be correct. While most abstention methods decide to withhold outputs before or after generation, dynamic mid-generation abstention considers early termination of unpromising reasoning traces at each token position. Prior work has explored empirical variants of this idea, but principled guidance for the abstention rule remains lacking. We present a formal analysis of dynamic abstention for LLMs, modeling abstention as an explicit action within a regularized reinforcement learning framework. An abstention reward parameter controls the trade-off between compute and information. We show that abstaining when the value function falls below this reward strictly outperforms natural baselines under general conditions. We further derive a principled and efficient method to approximate the value function. Empirical results on mathematical reasoning and toxicity avoidance tasks support our theory and demonstrate improved selective accuracy over existing methods.

</details>


### [126] [ProtoCLIP: Prototype-Aligned Latent Refinement for Robust Zero-Shot Chest X-Ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18444)
*Florian Kittler,Sheethal Bhat,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProtoCLIP是一种针对CLIP风格视觉语言模型的改进策略，通过有针对性的数据筛选和锚点对齐蒸馏，提升胸部X光片分类的零样本性能，解决了标签共现偏差、长尾类别不平衡和域转移不稳定等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本视觉语言模型在胸部X光片分类中存在三个主要限制：1）标签共现导致的混淆偏差；2）长尾类别不平衡；3）域转移下的不稳定性能。这些因素限制了模型在实际医疗应用中的可靠性和准确性。

Method: ProtoCLIP采用两种核心方法：1）构建以病理为中心的训练子集，通过精心筛选的负样本来减少共现偏差；2）引入表示保持的蒸馏目标，在保持语义结构的同时稳定模型适应过程，并提升对临床相关共现病理的区分能力。

Result: 在未见数据集VinDr-CXR上的评估显示，ProtoCLIP相比强大的CLIP基线在多个发现上AUC提升了2-10个百分点。特别是对于气胸检测，ProtoCLIP达到了0.94的AUC，达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: 研究表明，锚点引导的细化策略结合精心筛选的监督和受控适应，能够缓解医疗视觉语言模型中常见的零样本转移失败问题，且无需大规模重新训练。这为医疗影像分析中的零样本学习提供了有效的改进路径。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promise for chest radiograph classification, but their performance is often limited by confounding label co-occurrence, long-tail class imbalance, and transfer instability under domain shift. We propose ProtoCLIP, a refinement strategy for CLIP-style VLMs that improves zero-shot discrimination through targeted data curation and distilled anchor alignment. Specifically, we construct pathology-focused training subsets with curated negative samples to reduce co-occurrence bias. We also introduce a representation-preserving distillation objective to stabilize adaptation while maintaining semantic structure and improving discrimination of clinically relevant co-occurring pathologies. Evaluated on an unseen dataset VinDr-CXR, ProtoCLIP improves AUC by 2-10 percentage points over a strong CLIP-based baseline across multiple findings. For pneumothorax specifically, ProtoCLIP achieves a state-of-the-art AUC of 0.94. These results demonstrate that anchor-guided refinement, coupled with curated supervision and controlled adaptation, can mitigate common zero-shot transfer failures in medical VLMs without requiring large-scale retraining.

</details>


### [127] [AutoPPA: Automated Circuit PPA Optimization via Contrastive Code-based Rule Library Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18445)
*Chongxiao Li,Pengwei Jin,Di Huang,Guangrun Sun,Husheng Han,Jianan Mu,Xinyao Zheng,Jiaguo Zhu,Shuyi Xing,Hanjun Wei,Tianyun Ma,Shuyao Cheng,Rui Zhang,Ying Wang,Zidong Du,Qi Guo,Xing Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoPPA是一个完全自动化的PPA优化框架，通过自动生成优化规则来增强最优解的搜索，采用探索-评估-归纳工作流程，无需人工先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的PPA优化方法效率不足：反馈式方法缺乏先验知识，知识式方法过度依赖人工总结的优化规则。需要一种完全自动化的框架来生成有效的优化规则。

Method: 提出AutoPPA框架，采用探索-评估-归纳工作流程：从生成的多样化代码对中对比和抽象规则，而非依赖人工定义的知识。采用自适应多步搜索框架，为给定电路选择最有效的规则。

Result: 实验表明AutoPPA在性能、功耗和面积优化方面优于人工优化和当前最先进的方法SymRTLO和RTLRewriter。

Conclusion: AutoPPA通过自动生成优化规则的工作流程，实现了比现有方法更有效的PPA优化，无需依赖人工先验知识，具有更好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Performance, power, and area (PPA) optimization is a fundamental task in RTL design, requiring a precise understanding of circuit functionality and the relationship between circuit structures and PPA metrics. Recent studies attempt to automate this process using LLMs, but neither feedback-based nor knowledge-based methods are efficient enough, as they either design without any prior knowledge or rely heavily on human-summarized optimization rules.
  In this paper, we propose AutoPPA, a fully automated PPA optimization framework. The key idea is to automatically generate optimization rules that enhance the search for optimal solutions. To do this, AutoPPA employs an Explore-Evaluate-Induce ($E^2I$) workflow that contrasts and abstracts rules from diverse generated code pairs rather than manually defined prior knowledge, yielding better optimization patterns. To make the abstracted rules more generalizable, AutoPPA employs an adaptive multi-step search framework that adopts the most effective rules for a given circuit. Experiments show that AutoPPA outperforms both the manual optimization and the state-of-the-art methods SymRTLO and RTLRewriter.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Invariant Modality Representation for Robust Multimodal Learning from a Causal Inference Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18460)
*Sijie Mai,Shiqin Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果模态不变表示学习框架，通过解耦模态中的因果不变表示和环境特定伪相关表示，提升多模态情感计算的鲁棒性和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态情感计算模型容易学习到伪相关，在分布偏移或噪声模态下泛化能力差，需要更鲁棒的学习方法

Method: 提出因果模态不变表示学习框架，从因果推断视角将每个模态解耦为因果不变表示和环境特定伪相关表示，通过不变性约束、互信息约束和重构约束确保学习质量

Result: 在多个多模态基准测试中达到最先进性能，尤其在分布外数据和噪声数据上表现优异，验证了鲁棒性和泛化能力

Conclusion: CmIR框架通过因果解耦有效解决了多模态学习中的伪相关问题，为鲁棒多模态情感计算提供了理论和方法基础

Abstract: Multimodal affective computing aims to predict humans' sentiment, emotion, intention, and opinion using language, acoustic, and visual modalities. However, current models often learn spurious correlations that harm generalization under distribution shifts or noisy modalities. To address this, we propose a causal modality-invariant representation (CmIR) learning framework for robust multimodal learning. At its core, we introduce a theoretically grounded disentanglement method that separates each modality into `causal invariant representation' and `environment-specific spurious representation' from a causal inference perspective. CmIR ensures that the learned invariant representations retain stable predictive relationships with labels across different environments while preserving sufficient information from the raw inputs via invariance constraint, mutual information constraint, and reconstruction constraint. Experiments across multiple multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that CmIR achieves state-of-the-art performance. CmIR particularly excels on out-of-distribution data and noisy data, confirming its robustness and generalizability.

</details>


### [129] [Semantic Step Prediction: Multi-Step Latent Forecasting in LLM Reasoning Trajectories via Step Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18464)
*Yidi Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STP通过几何正则化改进LLM隐藏状态轨迹，研究发现采样位置对多步推理的语义结构有重要影响，在推理步骤边界应用STP比随机token采样效果更好


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语义Tube预测(STP)中采样位置如何影响多步推理的语义结构和几何影响，探索能否通过优化采样位置进一步提升推理性能

Method: 在连续语义推理步骤边界应用STP，与随机token采样的原始STP方法对比；使用学习到的非线性预测器探测潜在流形；分析语言建模损失对几何纯度的影响

Result: 在ProcessBench(3400样本)上，步骤边界STP比冻结基线多步潜在预测准确度提高168倍，而随机token STP仅提高4倍；STP形成的轨迹是平滑曲线而非直线；去除语言建模损失使轨迹MLP可预测性提高2倍

Conclusion: 采样位置是几何正则化的关键变量，多步潜在预测MSE可作为此类方法的新评估指标；生成质量与几何纯度之间存在权衡关系

Abstract: Semantic Tube Prediction (STP) leverages representation geometric to regularize LLM hidden-state trajectories toward locally linear geodesics during fine-tuning, thereby greatly improving data efficiency. The original STP recipe samples random token sub-spans, which is compatible with the base large language model (LLM) training architecture. Inspired by STP, we are interested to investigate whether the sampling position can further enhance the semantic structure of multi-step reasoning, and hence affect its geometric impact. We applied STP at consecutive semantic reasoning step boundaries and achieved 168x more accurate multi-step latent prediction than frozen baselines on ProcessBench (3,400 samples), compared to only 4x for the random-token STP. Probing the latent manifold with a learned non-linear predictor reveals that STP-shaped trajectories are smooth curves, not straight lines: a 3-layer MLP reduces prediction error by a further 3-12x over linear extrapolation on step-boundary models. Removing the language modeling loss yields trajectories that are 2x more MLP-predictable than the combined loss, revealing a tradeoff between generation quality and geometric purity. Our results identify sampling position as the critical variable in geometric regularization and establish multi-step latent prediction MSE as a new evaluation metric for this class of methods.

</details>


### [130] [An Integrated Deep-Learning Framework for Peptide-Protein Interaction Prediction and Target-Conditioned Peptide Generation with ConGA-PePPI and TC-PepGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18467)
*Chupei Tang,Junxiao Kong,Moyu Tang,Di Wang,Jixiu Zhai,Ronghao Xie,Shangkun Sima,Tianchi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 集成框架结合肽-蛋白相互作用预测模型(ConGA-PepPI)和目标条件肽生成模型(TC-PepGen)，实现早期肽筛选


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肽-蛋白相互作用(PepPIs)对细胞调控和肽疗法至关重要，但实验表征速度太慢。现有方法通常只关注相互作用预测或肽生成，缺乏候选优先排序、残基级解释和目标条件扩展的集成

Method: 开发集成框架：1) ConGA-PepPI使用非对称编码、双向交叉注意力和从配对预测到结合位点定位的渐进迁移；2) TC-PepGen通过层级条件在自回归解码中保持目标信息

Result: ConGA-PepPI在五折交叉验证中达到0.839准确率和0.921 AUROC，结合位点AUPR值蛋白侧0.601、肽侧0.950；TC-PepGen在长度条件基准测试中，40.39%的肽在AlphaFold 3 ipTM上超过天然模板

Conclusion: 该集成框架成功整合了肽-蛋白相互作用预测和肽生成，为早期肽筛选提供了有效工具，在预测准确性和生成肽质量方面均表现出色

Abstract: Motivation: Peptide-protein interactions (PepPIs) are central to cellular regulation and peptide therapeutics, but experimental characterization remains too slow for large-scale screening. Existing methods usually emphasize either interaction prediction or peptide generation, leaving candidate prioritization, residue-level interpretation, and target-conditioned expansion insufficiently integrated. Results: We present an integrated framework for early-stage peptide screening that combines a partner-aware prediction and localization model (ConGA-PepPI) with a target-conditioned generative model (TC-PepGen). ConGA-PepPI uses asymmetric encoding, bidirectional cross-attention, and progressive transfer from pair prediction to binding-site localization, while TC-PepGen preserves target information throughout autoregressive decoding via layerwise conditioning. In five-fold cross-validation, ConGA-PepPI achieved 0.839 accuracy and 0.921 AUROC, with binding-site AUPR values of 0.601 on the protein side and 0.950 on the peptide side, and remained competitive on external benchmarks. Under a controlled length-conditioned benchmark, 40.39% of TC-PepGen peptides exceeded native templates in AlphaFold 3 ipTM, and unconstrained generation retained evidence of target-conditioned signal.

</details>


### [131] [NI Sampling: Accelerating Discrete Diffusion Sampling by Token Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18471)
*Enshu Liu,Xuefei Ning,Yu Wang,Zinan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经指示器采样（NI Sampling）框架，通过优化离散扩散语言模型的token采样顺序实现显著加速，相比全步采样可达14.3倍加速且性能损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散语言模型的启发式采样策略效率低下，每次只选择少量token进行采样，存在巨大改进空间。研究表明充分利用每一步的正确预测可以大幅减少采样迭代次数而不影响准确性。

Method: 提出神经指示器采样（NI Sampling）框架，使用神经指示器决定每一步应该采样哪些token。设计了轨迹保持目标函数来训练指示器，确保优化采样顺序的同时保持生成质量。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型上的多基准测试表明，NI Sampling相比全步采样实现高达14.3倍加速且性能下降可忽略，在准确率-步数权衡中始终优于置信度阈值采样。

Conclusion: token采样顺序优化对离散扩散语言模型加速具有显著潜力，提出的NI Sampling框架通过神经指示器有效优化采样过程，实现了数量级的速度提升。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to traditional autoregressive approaches, offering the flexibility to generate tokens in arbitrary orders and the potential of parallel decoding. However, existing heuristic sampling strategies remain inefficient: they choose only a small part of tokens to sample at each step, leaving substantial room for improvement. In this work, we study the problem of token sampling order optimization and demonstrate its significant potential for acceleration. Specifically, we find that fully leveraging correct predictions at each step can reduce the number of sampling iterations by an order of magnitude without compromising accuracy. Based on this, we propose Neural Indicator Sampling (NI Sampling), a general sampling order optimization framework that utilize a neural indicator to decide which tokens should be sampled at each step. We further propose a novel trajectory-preserving objective to train the indicator. Experiments on LLaDA and Dream models across multiple benchmarks show that our method achieves up to 14.3$\times$ acceleration over full-step sampling with negligible performance drop, and consistently outperforms confidence threshold sampling in the accuracy-step trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/imagination-research/NI-Sampling.

</details>


### [132] [Train Separately, Merge Together: Modular Post-Training with Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18473)
*Jacob Morrison,Sanjay Adhikesaven,Akshita Bhagia,Matei Zaharia,Noah A. Smith,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BAR提出模块化训练框架，通过独立训练领域专家并组合为MoE架构，避免传统重训练的高成本和能力退化问题，实现线性扩展成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型扩展存在根本限制：从头重训练成本高昂且扩展性差，而持续训练往往导致现有能力退化。需要一种既能添加新领域能力又不损害已有能力的可扩展方法

Method: BAR框架包含三个核心组件：1) 独立训练领域专家（每个专家有自己的中期训练、监督微调和强化学习流程）；2) 通过轻量级路由器训练的混合专家架构组合专家；3) 支持独立更新专家而无需重新处理所有数据

Result: 在7B规模上，包含数学、代码、工具使用和安全四个领域专家的BAR系统获得49.1分（7个评估类别平均），匹配或超过重训练基线（无中期训练47.8分，有中期训练50.5分）。模块化训练避免了灾难性遗忘，更新成本线性扩展而非二次增长

Conclusion: 解耦的专家训练是扩展语言模型的可扩展替代方案，相比整体重训练具有结构优势：隔离各领域避免能力退化，显著降低更新或添加领域的成本和复杂性

Abstract: Extending a fully post-trained language model with new domain capabilities is fundamentally limited by monolithic training paradigms: retraining from scratch is expensive and scales poorly, while continued training often degrades existing capabilities. We present BAR (Branch-Adapt-Route), which trains independent domain experts, each through its own mid-training, supervised finetuning, and reinforcement learning pipeline, and composes them via a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with lightweight router training. Unlike retraining approaches that mix all domains and require full reprocessing for any update (with cost scaling quadratically), BAR enables updating individual experts independently with linear cost scaling and no degradation to existing domains. At the 7B scale, with experts for math, code, tool use, and safety, BAR achieves an overall score of 49.1 (averaged across 7 evaluation categories), matching or exceeding re-training baselines (47.8 without mid-training, 50.5 with). We further show that modular training provides a structural advantage: by isolating each domain, it avoids the catastrophic forgetting that occurs when late-stage RL degrades capabilities from earlier training stages, while significantly reducing the cost and complexity of updating or adding a domain. Together, these results suggest that decoupled, expert-based training is a scalable alternative to monolithic retraining for extending language models.

</details>


### [133] [Too Correct to Learn: Reinforcement Learning on Saturated Reasoning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18493)
*Zhenwen Liang,Yujun Zhou,Sidi Lu,Xiangliang Zhang,Haitao Mi,Dong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CUTS解码策略通过约束均匀采样解决RL微调中大模型在饱和基准上的模式崩溃问题，Mixed-CUTS训练框架结合探索与利用提升泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在强化学习微调中存在悖论：强大的基础模型在标准基准（如MATH）上已饱和，产生正确但同质的解决方案。这种环境下缺乏失败案例导致组相对算法（如GRPO）的优势信号消失，驱动策略陷入模式崩溃

Method: 提出CUTS（Constrained Uniform Top-K Sampling），一种无参数的解码策略，通过从约束的高置信度候选中均匀采样来强制结构保持探索。将其整合到Mixed-CUTS训练框架中，协同利用性和探索性rollout来放大组内优势方差

Result: 在Qwen3模型上的实验表明，该方法防止策略退化并显著提升跨域泛化能力。Mixed-CUTS在挑战性AIME25基准上将Pass@1准确率提升高达15.1%，优于标准GRPO

Conclusion: 在语义流形内保持多样性对于严谨推理至关重要，CUTS通过平坦化局部优化景观解决大模型RL微调中的模式崩溃问题，Mixed-CUTS框架有效提升推理泛化能力

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) enhances LLM reasoning, yet a paradox emerges as models scale: strong base models saturate standard benchmarks (e.g., MATH), yielding correct but homogeneous solutions. In such environments, the lack of failure cases causes the advantage signal in group-relative algorithms (e.g., GRPO) to vanish, driving policies into mode collapse. To address this, we propose Constrained Uniform Top-K Sampling (CUTS), a parameter-free decoding strategy enforcing structure-preserving exploration. Unlike standard sampling that follows model biases, CUTS flattens the local optimization landscape by sampling uniformly from constrained high-confidence candidates. We integrate this into Mixed-CUTS, a training framework synergizing exploitative and exploratory rollouts to amplify intra-group advantage variance. Experiments on Qwen3 models demonstrate that our approach prevents policy degeneration and significantly boosts out-of-domain generalization. Notably, Mixed-CUTS improves Pass@1 accuracy on the challenging AIME25 benchmark by up to 15.1% over standard GRPO, validating that maintaining diversity within the semantic manifold is critical for rigorous reasoning.

</details>


### [134] [IDOBE: Infectious Disease Outbreak forecasting Benchmark Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18521)
*Aniruddha Adiga,Jingyuan Chou,Anshul Chiranth,Bryan Lewis,Ana I. Bento,Shaun Truelove,Geoffrey Fox,Madhav Marathe,Harry Hochheiser,Srini Venkatramanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IDOBE是一个用于传染病暴发预测的标准化基准数据集，包含超过10,000个暴发事件的时间序列数据，覆盖13种疾病，用于评估和比较预测模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病暴发预测已成为实时疫情应对的重要组成部分，但目前缺乏标准化的基准数据集来评估统计和机器学习模型。此外，对于历史数据有限的新型疫情，这些方法的性能了解不足。

Method: IDOBE从多个数据源收集了一个多世纪的流行病学时间序列数据，覆盖美国各州和全球地点。使用基于导数的分割方法生成了超过10,000个暴发事件，涵盖13种疾病的病例和住院情况。采用信息论和分布度量来量化数据集的流行病学多样性，并使用11个基线模型进行多时间尺度短期预测（1-4周）。

Result: 研究发现基于MLP的方法具有最稳健的性能，而统计方法在疫情峰值前阶段略有优势。数据集和基线模型已在GitHub上公开，以支持标准化、可重复的暴发预测方法基准测试。

Conclusion: IDOBE提供了一个全面的流行病学暴发预测基准数据集，填补了该领域标准化评估工具的空白，有助于促进暴发预测方法的比较和改进。

Abstract: Epidemic forecasting has become an integral part of real-time infectious disease outbreak response. While collaborative ensembles composed of statistical and machine learning models have become the norm for real-time forecasting, standardized benchmark datasets for evaluating such methods are lacking. Further, there is limited understanding on performance of these methods for novel outbreaks with limited historical data. In this paper, we propose IDOBE, a curated collection of epidemiological time series focused on outbreak forecasting. IDOBE compiles from multiple data repositories spanning over a century of surveillance and across U.S. states and global locations. We perform derivative-based segmentation to generate over 10,000 outbreaks covering multiple outcomes such as cases and hospitalizations for 13 diseases. We consider a variety of information-theoretic and distributional measures to quantify the epidemiological diversity of the dataset. Finally, we perform multi-horizon short-term forecasting (1- to 4-week-ahead) through the progression of the outbreak using 11 baseline models and report on their performance. In addition to standard metrics such as NMSE and MAPE for point forecasts, we include probabilistic scoring rules such as Normalized Weighted Interval Score (NWIS) to quantify the performance. We find that MLP-based methods have the most robust performance, with statistical methods having a slight edge during the pre-peak phase. IDOBE dataset along with baselines are released publicly on https://github.com/NSSAC/IDOBE to enable standardized, reproducible benchmarking of outbreak forecasting methods.

</details>


### [135] [Wasserstein Distributionally Robust Risk-Sensitive Estimation via Conditional Value-at-Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18546)
*Feras Al Taha,Eilyan Bitar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分布鲁棒的风险敏感估计方法，在Wasserstein模糊集内最小化最坏情况CVaR，通过SDP可精确计算，在电价预测中表现优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统估计方法通常假设已知精确的概率分布，但在实际应用中分布往往未知或不确定。需要开发能够在分布不确定性下提供鲁棒性能的估计器，特别是在风险敏感的应用场景中。

Method: 采用分布鲁棒优化框架，将未知信号和观测的联合分布建模为属于给定type-2 Wasserstein球（模糊集）的分布。使用条件风险价值（CVaR）作为平方估计误差的性能度量。研究计算仿射估计器的问题，该估计器最小化给定模糊集内所有分布的最坏情况CVaR。当Wasserstein球中心的标称分布有限支撑时，通过求解可处理的半定规划（SDP）精确计算此类估计器。

Result: 理论证明当标称分布有限支撑时，最优仿射估计器可以通过求解半定规划精确计算。在批发电力价格预测任务中使用真实市场数据进行评估，结果显示所提估计器相比现有方法具有更低的样本外CVaR平方误差。

Conclusion: 提出的分布鲁棒风险敏感估计框架在存在分布不确定性时提供了有效的解决方案。通过Wasserstein模糊集和CVaR度量的结合，实现了对最坏情况风险的鲁棒性。理论上的可计算性和实际应用中的优越性能表明该方法在风险敏感估计问题中具有实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to risk-sensitive estimation of an unknown signal x from an observed signal y. The unknown signal and observation are modeled as random vectors whose joint probability distribution is unknown, but assumed to belong to a given type-2 Wasserstein ball of distributions, termed the ambiguity set. The performance of an estimator is measured according to the conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) of the squared estimation error. Within this framework, we study the problem of computing affine estimators that minimize the worst-case CVaR over all distributions in the given ambiguity set. As our main result, we show that, when the nominal distribution at the center of the Wasserstein ball is finitely supported, such estimators can be exactly computed by solving a tractable semidefinite program. We evaluate the proposed estimators on a wholesale electricity price forecasting task using real market data and show that they deliver lower out-of-sample CVaR of squared error compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [136] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文澄清了TurboQuant与早期DRIVE/EDEN量化方案的关系，指出TurboQuant是EDEN的特殊子集且性能较差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清TurboQuant与DRIVE/EDEN量化方案之间的关系，指出TurboQuant实际上是EDEN的特殊情况且存在多个次优设计

Method: 通过理论分析和实验验证，比较TurboQuant与EDEN的量化方案：1) TurboQuant_mse是固定S=1的EDEN特例；2) TurboQuant_prod结合了(b-1)位EDEN和1位QJL残差量化；3) 分析两者在随机旋转、Beta分布、Lloyd-Max算法等方面的相似性

Result: 实验表明：1) 带优化S的偏置EDEN比TurboQuant_mse更准确；2) 无偏EDEN显著优于TurboQuant_prod，通常超过1位精度（如2位EDEN优于3位TurboQuant_prod）；3) 在所有TurboQuant论文的实验设置中，EDEN均表现更优

Conclusion: TurboQuant是EDEN量化方案的特殊子集，其固定参数选择和量化结构设计存在多个次优之处，EDEN在理论和实验上均表现出更好的量化性能

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [137] [Latent Phase-Shift Rollback: Inference-Time Error Correction via Residual Stream Monitoring and KV-Cache Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18567)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LPSR是一种无需微调的推理时干预方法，通过监测残差流中的相位偏移来检测和纠正LLM推理错误，显著提升数学推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理过程中经常出现不可恢复的错误：一旦推理步骤出错，后续生成会加剧错误而非纠正。现有方法如提示自我校正效果不佳，甚至低于标准自回归生成

Method: 提出潜在相位偏移回滚(LPSR)：在关键层监测残差流，通过余弦相似度+熵双门检测方向突变（相位偏移），回滚KV缓存并注入预计算的转向向量。无需微调、梯度计算或额外前向传播

Result: 在MATH-500上，8B模型达到44.0%准确率，比标准AR(28.8%)提升15.2个百分点；显著优于提示自我校正(19.8%)和Best-of-16；性能超过标准70B模型但参数少8.75倍。发现检测-校正分离现象：错误检测最佳层(14)与任务准确率最佳层(16)不同

Conclusion: LPSR是一种高效、轻量的推理时干预方法，通过监测内部表示变化来纠正推理错误，显著提升数学推理性能，同时揭示了模型内部表示监测的最佳深度存在任务特异性

Abstract: Large language models frequently commit unrecoverable reasoning errors mid-generation: once a wrong step is taken, subsequent tokens compound the mistake rather than correct it. We introduce $\textbf{Latent Phase-Shift Rollback}$ (LPSR): at each generation step, we monitor the residual stream at a critical layer lcrit, detect abrupt directional reversals (phase shifts) via a cosine-similarity $+$ entropy dual gate, and respond by rolling back the KV-cache and injecting a pre-computed steering vector. No fine-tuning, gradient computation, or additional forward passes are required. LPSR achieves $\mathbf{44.0\%}$ on MATH-500 with an 8B model versus $28.8\%$ for standard AR ($+15.2$ pp; McNemar $χ^2 = 66.96$, $p < 10^{-15}$). Critically, prompted self-correction, the most natural inference-time baseline, scores only $19.8\%$, below standard AR; LPSR exceeds it by $+24.2$ pp ($χ^2 = 89.4$, $p \approx 0$). LPSR also outperforms Best-of-16 ($+7.8$ pp) at $5.4\times$ lower token cost, and surpasses a standard 70B model ($35.2\%$) with $8.75\times$ fewer parameters at ${\sim}3\times$ the token budget. A 32-layer sweep reveals a novel \textbf{detection-correction dissociation}: error-detection AUC peaks at layer~14 ($0.718$) but task accuracy peaks at layer~16 ($44.0\%$ vs.\ $29.2\%$), demonstrating that optimal monitoring depth differs for detection and correction.

</details>


### [138] [A multimodal and temporal foundation model for virtual patient representations at healthcare system scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18570)
*Andrew Zhang,Tong Ding,Sophia J. Wagner,Caiwei Tian,Ming Y. Lu,Rowland Pettit,Joshua E. Lewis,Alexandre Misrahi,Dandan Mo,Long Phi Le,Faisal Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Apollo是一个多模态时序基础模型，整合了30年医院记录中的28种医疗模态，创建了统一的患者表示，在322个临床预测和检索任务上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医学产生了大量多模态数据，但现有模型无法整合临床记录的完整广度和时间深度，缺乏统一的患者表示方法。

Method: 基于美国主要医院系统30多年的纵向医院记录（250亿条记录，720万患者），训练多模态时序基础模型，整合28种医疗模态和12个主要医学专科，学习包含10万+医疗事件的统一表示空间。

Result: 在322个预后和检索任务上评估：预测新疾病发病风险（95个任务）、疾病进展（78个）、治疗反应（59个）、治疗相关不良事件风险（17个）、医院运营终点（12个），模型预测与临床可解释的多模态生物标志物一致；在61个检索任务上表现良好，可作为多模态医学搜索引擎。

Conclusion: Apollo建立了可计算医学的基础，使患者护理的完整背景可被计算推理访问，为统一患者表示和临床预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern medicine generates vast multimodal data across siloed systems, yet no existing model integrates the full breadth and temporal depth of the clinical record into a unified patient representation. We introduce Apollo, a multimodal temporal foundation model trained and evaluated on over three decades of longitudinal hospital records from a major US hospital system, composed of 25 billion records from 7.2 million patients, representing 28 distinct medical modalities and 12 major medical specialties. Apollo learns a unified representation space integrating over 100 thousand unique medical events in our clinical vocabulary as well as images and clinical text. This "atlas of medical concepts" forms a computational substrate for modeling entire patient care journeys comprised of sequences of structured and unstructured events, which are compressed by Apollo into virtual patient representations. To assess the potential of these whole-patient representations, we created 322 prognosis and retrieval tasks from a held-out test set of 1.4 million patients. We demonstrate the generalized clinical forecasting potential of Apollo embeddings, including predicting new disease onset risk up to five years in advance (95 tasks), disease progression (78 tasks), treatment response (59 tasks), risk of treatment-related adverse events (17 tasks), and hospital operations endpoints (12 tasks). Using feature attribution techniques, we show that model predictions align with clinically-interpretable multimodal biomarkers. We evaluate semantic similarity search on 61 retrieval tasks, and moreover demonstrate the potential of Apollo as a multimodal medical search engine using text and image queries. Together, these modeling capabilities establish the foundation for computable medicine, where the full context of patient care becomes accessible to computational reasoning.

</details>


### [139] [Bounded Ratio Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18578)
*Yunke Ao,Le Chen,Bruce D. Lee,Assefa S. Wahd,Aline Czarnobai,Philipp Fürnstahl,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BRRL框架和BPO算法，弥合了PPO启发式目标与信任域方法理论基础之间的差距，确保单调性能改进，并在多个环境中优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO虽然成为主流策略优化算法，但其启发式裁剪目标与信任域方法理论基础之间存在显著脱节。本文旨在弥合这一差距，提供更坚实的理论基础。

Method: 提出BRRL框架，构建正则化约束策略优化问题并推导解析最优解。基于此开发BPO算法，最小化策略与BRRL解析最优解之间的优势加权散度。还扩展为GBPO用于LLM微调。

Result: BPO在MuJoCo、Atari和复杂IsaacLab环境（如Humanoid运动）中，GBPO在LLM微调任务中，通常在稳定性和最终性能上匹配或优于PPO和GRPO。

Conclusion: BRRL框架为策略优化提供了坚实的理论基础，BPO算法确保了单调性能改进，同时为PPO的成功提供了新的理论解释，并连接了信任域策略优化和交叉熵方法。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) has become the predominant algorithm for on-policy reinforcement learning due to its scalability and empirical robustness across domains. However, there is a significant disconnect between the underlying foundations of trust region methods and the heuristic clipped objective used in PPO. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing the Bounded Ratio Reinforcement Learning (BRRL) framework. We formulate a novel regularized and constrained policy optimization problem and derive its analytical optimal solution. We prove that this solution ensures monotonic performance improvement. To handle parameterized policy classes, we develop a policy optimization algorithm called Bounded Policy Optimization (BPO) that minimizes an advantage-weighted divergence between the policy and the analytic optimal solution from BRRL. We further establish a lower bound on the expected performance of the resulting policy in terms of the BPO loss function. Notably, our framework also provides a new theoretical lens to interpret the success of the PPO loss, and connects trust region policy optimization and the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). We additionally extend BPO to Group-relative BPO (GBPO) for LLM fine-tuning. Empirical evaluations of BPO across MuJoCo, Atari, and complex IsaacLab environments (e.g., Humanoid locomotion), and of GBPO for LLM fine-tuning tasks, demonstrate that BPO and GBPO generally match or outperform PPO and GRPO in stability and final performance.

</details>


### [140] [Sessa: Selective State Space Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18580)
*Liubomyr Horbatko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sessa是一种新的解码器架构，将注意力机制置于反馈路径中，实现递归多路径聚合，在长上下文任务中表现出色，同时保持短上下文语言建模的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型存在局限性：Transformer的自注意力在扩散注意力情况下，单个token的影响力会稀释为O(1/ℓ)；结构化状态空间模型（如Mamba）的长程敏感性会随延迟指数衰减。两者都只能通过单一路径检索或传播信息，缺乏灵活的选择性检索能力。

Method: 提出Sessa解码器，将注意力机制置于反馈路径内部，实现层内的递归多路径聚合。在特定假设下，Sessa能够实现幂律记忆尾迹O(ℓ^{-β})，其中0<β<1，比1/ℓ衰减更慢，并且在扩散均匀路由设置中达到紧界Θ(ℓ^{-β})。

Result: 在相同架构和训练预算下，Sessa在长上下文基准测试中表现最强，同时在短上下文语言建模中与Transformer和Mamba基线保持竞争力。只有Sessa在相同条件下实现了灵活的选择性检索，包括非衰减模式。

Conclusion: Sessa通过将注意力置于反馈路径中，实现了递归多路径聚合，克服了现有架构在长程信息传播和选择性检索方面的限制，为序列建模提供了新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Modern sequence models are dominated by Transformers, where self-attention mixes information from the visible context in an input-dependent way. However, when retrieval is not sharp and attention remains diffuse over an effective support $S_{\mathrm{eff}}(t)$, the influence of any individual token is diluted, typically scaling as $O(1/S_{\mathrm{eff}}(t))$ and reaching $O(1/\ell)$ for old tokens in full-prefix settings. Structured state-space models process sequences recurrently through an explicit feedback path; selective variants such as Mamba make this feedback input-dependent, yet when freeze time cannot be sustained over long intervals, their long-range sensitivity decays exponentially with lag. Existing architectures therefore either retrieve from the past in a single read or propagate information through a single feedback chain. We introduce Sessa, a decoder that places attention inside a feedback path, enabling recurrent many-path aggregation within a layer. Under stated assumptions, Sessa admits regimes with a power-law memory tail in lag $\ell$ of order $O(\ell^{-β})$ for $0<β<1$, which is asymptotically slower than $1/\ell$; moreover, this rate is tight in an explicit diffuse uniform-routing setting where the influence is $Θ(\ell^{-β})$. Under the same conditions, only Sessa among the compared model classes realizes flexible selective retrieval, including non-decaying profiles. Empirically, under matched architectures and training budgets, Sessa achieves the strongest performance on our long-context benchmarks while remaining competitive with Transformer and Mamba style baselines on short-context language modeling.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Cooperative Coevolution versus Monolithic Evolutionary Search for Semi-Supervised Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16412)
*Jamal Toutouh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种合作协同进化方法CC-SSL，用于极端低标签率下的半监督表格分类，通过协同进化特征子集视图和伪标签策略，在1%标签数据下相比轻量级基线获得更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极端低标签率（如1%、5%、10%标签数据）下的半监督表格分类问题，现有轻量级方法在此场景下性能有限，需要更有效的半监督学习策略。

Method: 提出合作协同进化方法CC-SSL，协同进化两个特征子集视图和伪标签策略；同时设计了匹配的单体进化基线EA-SSL作为对比；在25个OpenML数据集上评估，使用MacroF1和准确率指标。

Result: CC-SSL和EA-SSL在测试MacroF1中位数上优于三个轻量级基线，最大差距出现在1%标签数据时；CC-SSL与EA-SSL在最终测试性能上大多统计无差异；EA-SSL在搜索中显示更高的最佳适应度和多样性。

Conclusion: 合作协同进化方法在极端低标签率表格分类中有效，与单体进化方法性能相当但搜索特性不同；进化方法相比轻量级基线在低标签率下具有优势。

Abstract: This paper studies semi-supervised tabular classification in the extreme low-label regime using lightweight base learners. The paper proposes a cooperative coevolutionary method (CC-SSL) that evolves (i) two feature-subset views and (ii) a pseudo-labeling policy, and compares it to a matched monolithic evolutionary baseline (EA-SSL) and three lightweight SSL baselines. Experiments on 25 OpenML datasets with labeled fractions {1%,5%,10%} evaluate test MacroF1 and accuracy, together with evolutionary and pseudo-label diagnostics. CC-SSL and EA-SSL achieve higher median test MacroF1 than the lightweight baselines, with the largest separations at 1% labeled data. Most CC-SSL vs. EA-SSL comparisons are statistical draws on final test performance. EA-SSL shows higher best-so-far fitness and higher diversity during search, while time-to-target is comparable and generations-to-target favors EA-SSL in several multiclass settings. Pseudo-label volume, ProbeDrop, and validation optimism show no significant differences between CC-SSL and EA-SSL under the shared protocol.

</details>


### [142] [Breaking Validity-Induced Boundaries to Expand Algorithm Search Space: A Two-Stage AST-Based Operator for LLM-Driven Automated Heuristic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16420)
*Sun Shengming,Shi Jialong*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于结构的两阶段进化算子，用于LLM自动启发式设计，通过AST操作生成多样化结构变体，再用LLM修复为可执行代码，提升搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-AHD框架主要依赖语义进化算子，完全基于LLM预训练知识，采用单阶段方法要求生成代码必须有效，且通常依赖"思想-代码"表示。这种端到端生成方式从根本上限制了算法搜索空间的探索能力。

Method: 提出两阶段、基于结构的进化算子：第一阶段直接在启发式代码的抽象语法树(AST)上执行交叉和变异，有意生成多样化但通常无效的结构变体；第二阶段使用LLM将这些无效启发式修复为可执行、高质量的代码。根据底层框架，原始无效变体或修复后的启发式被整合到种群中以保留潜在结构模式。

Result: 实验证明该算子能显著提升最先进LLM-AHD算法（如EoH-S）的搜索能力。在旅行商问题(TSP)和在线装箱问题(OBP)上的实验结果表明，该方法有效提高了优化性能和收敛速度。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段结构进化算子通过分离结构探索和语义修复，克服了传统LLM-AHD方法的局限性，增强了算法搜索空间的探索能力，为自动启发式设计提供了更有效的进化策略。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) based automated heuristic design (AHD) has shown great potential in discovering efficient heuristics. Most existing LLM-AHD frameworks use semantic evolutionary operators that rely entirely on the LLM's pre-trained knowledge. These one-stage methods strictly require the generated code to be valid during the operation and often rely on a ``thought-code'' representation. We argue that this end-to-end generation fundamentally limits the exploration ability within the algorithm search space.
  In this paper, we propose a two-stage, structure-based evolutionary operator for LLM-AHD. In the first stage, our approach directly performs crossover and mutation on the Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) of the heuristic code, intentionally generating diverse but often invalid structural variants. In the second stage, the LLM is employed to repair these invalid heuristics into executable, high-quality code. Depending on the underlying framework, either the raw invalid variants or the repaired heuristics are integrated into the population to preserve potential structural patterns. We demonstrate that the proposed operator can significantly enhance the search ability of state-of-the-art LLM-AHD algorithms, such as EoH-S. Experimental results on the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) and the Online Bin Packing Problem (OBP) show that our method effectively improves both optimization performance and convergence speed.

</details>


### [143] [Spike-driven Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16475)
*Han Xu,Xuerui Qiu,Baiyu Chen,Xinhao Luo,Xingrun Xing,Jiahong Zhang,Bo Lei,Tiejun Huang,Bo Xu,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: SDLLM是一种基于脉冲驱动的大型语言模型，通过稀疏加法操作替代密集矩阵乘法，显著降低推理能耗，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大脑信息处理机制的启发，探索如何将脉冲驱动特性有效整合到LLM推理中。现有SNN与Transformer结合的方法难以实现数十亿参数的脉冲驱动LLM，且现有脉冲编码方案在LLM级别存在表示能力有限和稀疏性问题。

Method: 提出SDLLM模型，采用即插即用的gamma-SQP两步脉冲编码方法，确保量化过程与模型语义空间对齐；引入对称量化下的双向编码和膜电位裁剪机制，显著降低脉冲发放率，同时将时间步数减半。

Result: SDLLM显著降低推理成本，在脉冲范式下达到最先进的任务性能。相比之前的脉冲LLM，能耗降低7倍，准确率提升4.2%。

Conclusion: SDLLM为下一代事件驱动神经形态芯片的架构设计提供了启发，展示了通过稀疏加法操作替代密集矩阵乘法实现高效脉冲驱动LLM的可行性。

Abstract: Current Large Language Models (LLMs) are primarily based on large-scale dense matrix multiplications. Inspired by the brain's information processing mechanism, we explore the fundamental question: how to effectively integrate the brain's spiking-driven characteristics into LLM inference. Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) possess spike-driven characteristics, and some works have attempted to combine SNNs with Transformers. However, achieving spike-driven LLMs with billions of parameters, relying solely on sparse additions, remains a challenge in the SNN field. To address the issues of limited representational capacity and sparsity in existing spike encoding schemes at the LLM level, we propose SDLLM, a spike-driven large language model that eliminates dense matrix multiplications through sparse addition operations. Specifically, we use the plug-and-play gamma-SQP two-step spike encoding method to ensure that the quantization process aligns with the model's semantic space, mitigating representation degradation caused by binary spikes. Furthermore, we introduce bidirectional encoding under symmetric quantization and membrane potential clipping mechanisms, leading to spike trains with no or low firing counts dominating, significantly reducing the model's spike firing rate, while halving the number of time steps. Experimental results show that SDLLM not only significantly reduces inference costs but also achieves state-of-the-art task performance under the spike-based paradigm. For example, compared to previous spike-based LLMs, SDLLM reduces energy consumption by 7x and improves accuracy by 4.2%. Our model provides inspiration for the architecture design of the next generation of event-driven neuromorphic chips.

</details>


### [144] [Monotone but Exciting: On Evolving Monotone Boolean Functions with High Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17342)
*Claude Carlet,Marko Čupić,Marko Ðurasevic,Domagoj Jakobovic,Luca Mariot,Stjepan Picek*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 进化计算用于演化具有高非线性度的单调布尔函数，比较三种编码方式在平衡和非平衡设置下的表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调布尔函数在结构上很重要，但其受限形式限制了可实现的非线性度。研究进化计算能否演化出具有高非线性度的单调布尔函数，包括平衡和非平衡设置。

Method: 使用三种编码方式：标准真值表表示、保持汉明权重的平衡真值表编码、符号树遗传编程表示。引入非单调性惩罚，结合针对平衡性和非线性度的适应度函数。实验维度从n=5到n=14。

Result: 进化搜索能够发现非线性度明显超过多数函数的单调布尔函数，在某些情况下接近目前已知的最佳值。不同编码方式表现差异显著：平衡真值表编码在较大维度上表现较差，而标准真值表和遗传编程编码保持竞争力，遗传编程在最大测试维度中尤其相关。

Conclusion: 进化计算可以有效演化具有高非线性度的单调布尔函数，编码选择对性能有重要影响，遗传编程在大维度问题上表现出优势。

Abstract: Monotone Boolean functions are a structurally important class of Boolean functions, but their restricted form imposes strong limitations on achievable nonlinearity. In this paper, we investigate whether evolutionary computation can evolve monotone Boolean functions with high nonlinearity, both in the balanced and imbalanced settings. We consider three solution encodings: the standard truth table representation, a balanced truth table encoding that preserves Hamming weight, and a symbolic tree-based genetic programming representation. To guide the search toward monotone increasing functions, we introduce a non-monotonicity penalty and combine it with fitness functions targeting balancedness and nonlinearity. Experimental results are reported for dimensions from $n=5$ to $n=14$. The results show that evolutionary search can discover monotone Boolean functions with nonlinearities clearly exceeding those of majority functions, and in several cases approaching the best currently known values for monotone functions. At the same time, the experiments reveal substantial differences between encodings: the balanced truth table encoding performs poorly for larger dimensions, while the standard truth table and genetic programming encodings remain competitive, with genetic programming becoming especially relevant in the largest tested dimensions.

</details>


### [145] [Similarity-based Portfolio Construction for Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18196)
*Catalin-Viorel Dinu,Diederick Vermetten,Carola Doerr*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文提出在固定预算的黑盒优化中使用算法组合策略，通过将评估预算分配给多个算法运行来减少方差并利用算法互补性，优于传统的单一算法选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑盒优化中，为未知问题选择单一算法存在风险：选择器不准确可能导致选择不当，即使性能良好的算法也可能因高方差而在单次运行中表现不佳。需要一种更稳健的方法来处理算法选择的不确定性。

Method: 提出序列算法组合策略，将评估预算分配给多个算法的运行。首先展示基于完整训练集的朴素组合已优于传统基线（虚拟最佳求解器），然后提出基于k近邻的微调方法，为未见实例构建定制化组合。

Result: 朴素算法组合已能超越最强的传统基线（虚拟最佳求解器）。基于k近邻的微调方法进一步提升了性能，证明了在固定预算黑盒优化中组合选择的有效性。

Conclusion: 算法组合策略通过方差减少和算法互补性，在固定预算黑盒优化中优于单一算法选择方法，为算法选择问题提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: In black-box optimization, a central question is which algorithm to use to solve a given, previously unseen, problem. Selecting a single algorithm, however, entails inherent risks: inaccuracies in the selector may lead to poor choices, and even well-performing algorithms with high variance can yield unsatisfactory results in a single run. A natural remedy is to split the evaluation budget across multiple runs of potentially different algorithms. Such sequential algorithm portfolios benefit from variance reduction and complementarities between algorithms, often outperforming approaches that allocate the entire budget to a single solver.
  While effective portfolios can be constructed post-hoc, transferring this idea to the algorithm selection setting is non-trivial. We show that a naive portfolio constructed over the full training set already outperforms the strongest traditional baseline, the virtual best solver. We then propose a simple yet effective k-nearest-neighbor-based finetuning approach to construct portfolios tailored to unseen instances, yielding further improvements and highlighting the effectiveness of portfolio selection in fixed-budget black-box optimization.

</details>


### [146] [Neutrally Evolving Interlocking Complexity in the Quandary Den](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18361)
*Andrew Walsh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 引入Quandary Den人工生命模型探索中性进化场景，揭示在无需更多信息需求的情况下复杂性如何通过亚功能化和掩蔽两种机制自发增加


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生物学中许多蛋白质复合物以连锁方式协调完成不同功能。虽然适应性进化可以解释部分复杂性，但在中性解释足够时不应将其作为默认解释。需要探索在缺乏更大信息需求时复杂性如何通过中性进化自发增加

Method: 引入新的"Quandary Den"人工生命模型，设计中性进化场景，观察在缺乏适应性压力时复杂性的自发形成机制

Result: 发现两种连锁复杂性场景：1)亚功能化导致功能在复合物中扩散；2)掩蔽允许复合物内干扰在遗传上积累，需要在表达层面进行阻断

Conclusion: 复杂性可以在没有适应性优势的情况下通过中性进化自发产生，亚功能化和掩蔽是两种重要的中性机制，挑战了复杂性必然源于适应性进化的默认假设

Abstract: Molecular biology features numerous complexes of proteins that coordinate in an interlocking fashion to fulfill different functions. Adaptive evolution explains some of this complexity, but needn't be the default when neutral explanations suffice. A new artificial life model ``organism,'' the Quandary Den, is introduced to explore different neutral evolution scenarios where complexity increases in the absence of greater informational needs. Two interlocking complexity scenarios emerge. Subfunctionalization leads to functionality diffusing through the complex. Masking allows intracomplex interference to accumulate genetically, requiring that it be blocked at the level of expression.

</details>
